# Kirfel, Das Purāṇa Pañcalakṣaṇa (Bonn 1927)
# Textgruppe I

# constituted text only; critical apparatus removed


## 1. Abschnitt. Sarga und Pratisarga  (A. 17.6b-17; Br. 1.31-56; H. 27-53; Śidh. 51.3-28.)

śṛṇudhvaṃ tāṃ kathāṃ divyāṃ sarvapāpapramocinīṃ |
kathyamānāṃ mayā citrāṃ bahvarthāṃ śrutivistarāṃ. || 1 ||
yas cemāṃ dhārayen nityaṃ śṛṇuyād vāpy abhikṣṇaśaḥ, |
svavaṃśadhāraṇaṃ kṛtvā svargaloke mahīyate. || 2 ||
avyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ yat, tan nityaṃ sadasadātmakaṃ |
pradhānaṃ, puruṣas tasmān nirmame viśvam īśvaraḥ. || 3 ||
taṃ budhyadhvaṃ muniśreṣṭhā brahmāṇaṃ amitaujasaṃ |
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇaṃ. || 4 ||
ahamkāras tu mahatas, tasmād bhutāni jajñire, bhūtabhedāś ca bhūtebhya, iti sargaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 5 ||
vistarāvyavayaṃ caiva yathāprajñāṃ yathāśruti |
kīrtyamānaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai pūrveṣāṃ kīrtivardhanaṃ! || 6 ||
dhanyaṃ yaśasyaṃ śatrughnaṃ svargyam āyuḥpravardhanaṃ |
kīrtanaṃ sthirakīrtināṃ sarveṣāṃ puṇyakarmaṇāṃ. |
yasmāt kalpāyate kalpaḥ samagraṃ śucaye śuciḥ, || 7 ||
a vṛṣṇivamśad vaksyāmi bhūtasargam anuttamaṃ || 8 ||
tasmai hiranyagarbhāya puruṣāyeśvarāya ca |
ajāya sthānurūpāya varisṭhāya prajābhuve |
namaskṛtya pravaksyāmi bhuvaḥ sargam anuttamaṃ. || 8 ||
brahmā sraṣṭā, hariḥ pātā saṃhartā ca maheśvaraḥ, |
tasya sargasya nānyo 'sti kāle kāle yathā gate. || 9 ||
tataḥ svayambhūr bhagavān sisṛksur vividhāḥ prajāḥ |
apā eva sasarjādau, tāsu vīryam avāsṛjat. || 10 ||
āpo nārā iti proktā, āpo vai narasūnavaḥ, |
ayanaṃ tasya tāḥ pūrvaṃ, tena nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ. || 11 ||
hiranyavarnam abhavat tad aṇḍam udakeśayaṃ, |
tatra jajñe svayaṃ brahmā svayambhūr iti naḥ śrutaṃ. || 12 ||
hiranyagarbho bhagavān uṣitvā parivatsaraṃ |
tad aṇḍam akarod dvaidhaṃ, divaṃ bhuvam athāpi ca. || 13 ||
[adho 'thordhvaṃ prayuktāni bhuvanāni caturdaśa. |]
tayoḥ śakalayor madhya ākāśam asṛjat prabhuḥ, |
apsu pāriplavāṃ pṛthvīṃ, diśāś ca daśadhā dadhe. || 14 ||
tatra kālaṃ mano vācaṃ kāmaṃ krodham atho ratiṃ |
sasarja sṛṣṭiṃ tadrūpāṃ sraṣṭum icchan prajāpatiḥ. || 15 ||
marīcim atṛy-aṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratuṃ |
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahātejāḥ so 'sṛjat sapta mānasān. || 16 ||
sapta brahmāṇa ity ete purāṇe niścayaṃ gatāḥ |
nārāyaṇātmakānāṃ vai saptānāṃ brahmajanmanāṃ. || 17 ||
tato 'sṛjat punar brahmā rudraṃ roṣātmasaṃbhavaṃ |
sanatkumāraṃ ca vibhuṃ pūrveṣām api pūrvajaṃ. || 18 ||
saptaite janayanti sma prajā, rudraś ca bho dvijāḥ |
ataḥ sanatkumārās ca tejaḥ saṃkṣipya tiṣṭhataḥ. || 19 ||
teṣāṃ sapta mahāvaṃśā divyā devagaṇānvitāḥ |
kriyāvantaḥ prajāvanto maharṣibhir alamkṛṭāḥ. || 20 ||
vidyuto 'śanimeghāṃś ca rohitendradhanūṃṣi ca |
vayāṃsi ca sasarjādau parjanyaṃ ca sasarja ha. || 21 ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni nirmame yajñasiddhaye, |
sādhyās tair ayajan devān, ity evam anuṣūsuma. || 22 ||
mukhād devān ajanayat pitṛmś caivātha vakṣasaḥ |
prajanāc ca manuṣyān vai, jaghaṇān nirmame 'suran. || 22 ||
uccāvacāni bhūtāni gātrebhyas tasya jajñire |
āpavasya prajāsargaṃ sṛjato hi prajāpateḥ. || 23 ||
sṛjyamānāḥ prajā naiva vivardhante yadā, tadā |
dvidhā kṛtvātmano deham ardhena puruṣo 'bhavat, || 24 ||
ardhena nārī, tasyāṃ sa sasṛje vividhāḥ prajāḥ; |
divaṃ ca pṛthivīṃ caiva mahimnā vyāpya tiṣṭhati. || 25 ||
virājam asṛjad viṣṇuḥ, so 'sṛjat puruṣaṃ virāt, |
puruṣaṃ taṃ manuṃ vidyād, tasya manvantaraṃ smṛtaṃ. || 26 ||
dviṭiyaṃ mānasasyaivaṃ manor antaram ucyate. |
sa vairājāḥ prajāsargaṃ sasarja puruṣaḥ prabhuḥ. || 27 ||
nārāyaṇavisargasya prajās tasyāpy ayonijāḥ. |
āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyāḥ prajāvāmś ca bhaven naraḥ, || 28 ||
ādisargaṃ viditvemaṃ yathestāṃ prāpnuyād gatiṃ. || 29 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 1. Kapitel

samśṛṣṭāsu prajāsv evam āpavo vai prajāpatiḥ |
lebhe vai puruṣaḥ patniṃ śatarūpām ayonijāṃ. || 1 ||
āpavasya mahimnā tu divam āvṛtya tiṣṭhataḥ |
dharmenaiva munisreṣṭhaḥ śatarūpā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
sā tu varṣaśataṃ taptvā tapaḥ paramaduścaraṃ |
bhartāraṃ dīptatapasaṃ puruṣaṃ pratyapadyata. || 1 ||
sa vai svāyambhuvo viprāḥ puruṣo manur ucyate. |
tasyaikasaptatiyugaṃ manvantaram ihocyate. || 1 ||
vairājāt puruṣād viraṃ śatarūpā vyajāyata, |
priyavrat-ottānapādaṃ virāt kāmyā vyajāyata. || 1 ||
kāmyā nāma sutā śreṣṭhā kardamasya prajāpateḥ, |
sūta uvāca: cākṣuṣasya nisargaṃ tu samāsāc chrotum arhatha |
yasyānvavāye saṃbhūtaḥ pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān |
prajānāṃ patayaś cānye dakṣaḥ prācetasas tathā. || 1 ||
kāmyāputrās ca catvāraḥ: samrāṭ kuksīr virāṭ prabhuḥ. || 16 ||
uttānapādaṃ jagrāha putram atriḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
dattakaḥ sa tu putro 'sya rājā hy āsit prajāpatiḥ, || 21 ||
svāyaṃbhuvena manunā datto 'treḥ kāraṇaṃ prati. |
manvantaram athāsādyā bhaviṣyac cākṣuṣasya ha || 22 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tad anuvakṣyāmi upodghātena vai dvijāḥ. |
uttānapādāc caturā sūnṛtā suṣuve sutān || 2 ||
dharmasya kanyā suśroṇī sūnṛtā nāma viśrutā, |
utpannā vājimedhena, dhruvasya jananī śubhā. || 3 ||
dharmasya patnyāṃ lakṣmyāṃ vai utpannā sā śucismitā. |
dhruvaṃ ca kirtimantaṃ ca ayuṣmantaṃ vasuṃ tathā |
uttānapādo 'janayat uttānapādo 'janayat sūnṛtāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 4 ||
kanye dve ca śucismite: || 4 ||
dhruvo varṣasahasraṇi trīṇi divyāni vīryavān |
tapas tepe nirāharaḥ, prārthayan sumahad yaśaḥ. || 5 ||
tasmai brahmā dadau prītaḥ sthānam ātmasamaṃ prabhuḥ, |
acalaṃ caiva purataḥ saptarṣiṇāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 6 ||
tasyābhimānaṃ ṛddhiṃ ca mahimānaṃ nirikṣya ca |
devāsurāṇām ācāryaḥ ślokaṃ apy uśanā jagau: || 7 ||
aho 'sya tapaso vīryam, aho śrutam, aho 'dbhutaṃ, |
yam adya purataḥ kṛtvā dhruvaṃ saptarṣayaḥ sthitāḥ! || 8 ||
tasmāc chīṣṭiṃ ca bhavyaṃ ca dhruvāc chambhur vyajāyata. |
śiṣṭer adhatta succhāyā pañca putrān akalmaṣān: || 9 ||
ripuṃ ripumjayaṃ vipraṃ vṛkalaṃ vṛkatejasaṃ |
ripor adhatta bṛhatī cākṣuṣaṃ ajījanat puṣkarinyāṃ vairanyāṃ cākṣuṣo manuṃ |
prajāpater ātmajāyāṃ viranasya mahātmanaḥ. || 11 ||
manor ajāyanta daśa nadvalāyāṃ mahaujasaḥ |
kaṇyāyāṃ sumahavīryā vairājasya prajāpateḥ: || 12 ||
ūruḥ pūruḥ śatadyumnas tapasvī satyavāk kavīḥ |
agniṣṭud atirātraś ca sudyumnaś ceti te nava || 13 ||
abhimanyuś ca daśamo nadvalāyāṃ mahaujasaḥ. |
ūror ajanayat putrān śadāgneyī mahāprabhān: || 14 ||
aṅgaṃ sumanasaṃ svātiṃ kratum aṅgirasaṃ gayaṃ. |
aṅgāt sunithāpatyaṃ vai venam ekaṃ vyajāyata. || 15 ||
apacāreṇa venasya prakopaḥ sumahān abhūt, |
prajārthaṃ ṛṣayo 'thāsyā mamanthur dakṣiṇaṃ karaṃ. || 16 ||
venasya mathite pāṇau saṃbabhūva mahān nṛpaḥ. |
taṃ dṛṣtvā munayaḥ prāhur : | vainyo nāma mahīpālo, esa vai muditāḥ prajāḥ || 17 || yaḥ pṛthuḥ parikīrtitāḥ, || 17 ||
kariṣyati mahāteja yaśāś ca prāpsyate mahat. |
sa dhanvī kavacī jātas, tejasā nirdahan iva. || 18 ||
pṛthur vainyas tadā cemāṃ rarakṣa kṣatrapūrvajaḥ, |
rājasūyābhiṣiktānām ādyaḥ sa vasudhādhipaḥ. || 19 ||
tasmāc caiva samutpannau nipuṇau sūta-māgadhaḥ. |
teneyaṃ gaur mahardjñā dugdhā sasyāni dhīmatā || 20 ||
prajānāṃ vṛttikāmena devaiḥ sarṣigaṇaiḥ saha |
pitṛbhir dānavaiṣ caiva gandharvaiḥ sāpsarogaṇaiḥ || 21 ||
sarpaiḥ puṇyajanaiṣ caiva vīrudbhiḥ parvatais tathā; |
teṣu teṣu ca pātreṣu duhyamānā vasuṃdharā || 22 ||
prādād yathepsitaṃ kṣīraṃ, tena prāṇān adhārayan. |
pṛthos tu putrau dharmajñau jajñāte 'ntardhi-pālinau. || 23 ||
śikhaṇḍini havirdhānam antardhānād vyajāyata, |
havirdhānāt śaḍāgneyi dhiṣaṇājanayat sutān: || 24 ||
prācinabarhiṣaṃ śukraṃ gayaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vraj-ājinau. |
prācinabarbir bhagavān mahān āsit prajāpatiḥ || 25 ||
havirdhānān mahārājo, yena samvardhitāḥ prajāḥ. |
prācināgrāḥ kuśās tasya pṛthivyāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 26 ||
prācinabarbir bhagavān pṛthivītalacāriṇāḥ. |
balaśrutatapoviryaiḥ pṛthivyāṃ ekaṛāḍ asau. |
prācināgrāḥ kuśās tasya tasmāt prācinabarhy asau. || 26 ||
samudratanayāyāṃ tu kṛtadāro 'bhavat prabhuḥ || 27 ||
mahatas tapasaḥ pāre savarnāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. |
savarnādhatta sāmudri daśa prācinabarhiṣaḥ, || 28 ||
sarvān pracetaso nāma dhanurvedasya pāragān. |
apṛthagdharmacaranās te 'tapyanta mahat tapaḥ || 29 ||
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi samudrasalileśayāḥ. |
tapas̄ caratsu pṛthivīṃ pracetaḥsu mahīruhāḥ || 30 ||
arakṣya'mānām āvavru, babhūvātha prajāksayaḥ. |
pratyāhṛte tadā tasmin cāksuṣasyāntare manoḥ || 31 ||
nāśakan māruto vātuṃ, vṛtaṃ kham abhavad drumaiḥ; |
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi na śekus̄ ceṣṭituṃ prajāḥ. || 32 ||
tad upāsrutya tapasā yuktāḥ sarve pracetasaḥ |
mukhebhyo vāyum agnim̄ ca sasṛjur jātamanyavaḥ. || 33 ||
unmūlān atha tān vṛksān kṛtvā vāyur aśoṣayat, |
tān agnir adahad ghora, evam āsīd drumakṣayaḥ. || 34 ||
druma'kṣayam atho buddhvā kim̄cicchiṣṭesu śākbisu |
upagamyābravīd etān rājā somaḥ̄ prajāpatin: || 35 ||
kopaṃ yacchata rājānaḥ sarve prācīnabarhiṣaḥ, |
vṛkṣaśūnyā kṛtā pṛthvī, śāmyetām agnimārutau! || 36 ||
yuṣmākaṃ tejaso 'ṛdhena mama cārdhena tejasaḥ |
asyām utpatsyate vidvān dakṣo nāma prajāpatiḥ. || 39 ||
sa imāṃ dagdhabhūyiṣṭhāṃ yuṣmattejomayena vai |
agnināgnisamo bhūyaḥ prajāḥ samvardhayiṣyati. || 40 ||
tataḥ somasya vacanāj jagṛhus te pracetasaḥ |
samhṛtya kopaṃ vṛkṣebhyaḥ patniṃ dharmeṇa māriṣāṃ. || 41 ||
māriṣāyāṃ tatas te vai manasā garbham ādadhuḥ. |
daśabhyas tu pracetobhyo māriṣāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ || 42 ||
dakṣo jajñe mahatejāḥ somasyāṃśena vīryavān. |
putrān utpādayāmāsa | asṛjan manasā tvādau somavamśavivardhanān. || 43 || prajā dakṣo na maithunāt. || 43 ||
acarāṃś ca carāṃś caiva dvipado 'tha catuspadaḥ. |
sa sṛṣtvā manasā dakṣaḥ paścād asṛjata striyaḥ. || 44 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
śiṣṭāḥ somāya rājñe ca | kālasya nayane yuktāḥ nakṣatrākhyaḥ dadau prabhuḥ. || saptavimśatim indave. || 45 ||
ebhyo dattvā tato 'nyā vai catasro 'riṣṭanemine, |
dve caiva bahuputrāya dve caiv-āṅgirase tathā, |
kaṇyām ekāṃ kṛṣāśvāya, tebhyo 'patyaṃ nibodhata! || 45 ||
antaraṃ cākṣuṣasyātra manoḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu gīyate |
manor vaivasvatasyāpi saptamasya prajāpateḥ. || 45 ||
tāsu devāḥ khagā gāvo nāgā ditiṣa-dānavāḥ |
gandharvāpsarasaś caiva jajñire 'nyāś ca jātayaḥ. || 46 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti loke 'smīn prajā maithunasambhavaḥ, |
saṃkalpād darśanāt sparśāt pūrvveṣāṃ sṛṣṭir ucyate. || 47 ||
munaya (ṛśaya) ūcuḥ̃: devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
saṃbhavaḥ kathitaḥ̃ pūrvam̃ dakṣasya ca mahātmanaḥ̃. || 48 ||
aṅguṣṭhād brahmano jāto | prāṇāt prajāpater janma dakṣaḥ̃ proktas tvayānagha | dakṣasya kathitam̃ tvayā. || 49 ||
vāmāṅguṣṭhāt tathā caiva tasya patnī vyajāyata. || 49 ||
katham̃ prācetasatvam̃ sa punar lebhe mahātapāḥ̃, |
etam̃ naḥ saṃśayam̃ sūta vyākhyātum̃ tvam̃ ihārhasi, |
dauhitraś caiva somasya katham̃ śvaśuratām̃ gataḥ? || 50 ||
(lomaharṣaṇa) sūta uvāca: utpattiś ca nirodhaś ca nityam̃ bhūtesu varcate, |
ṛśayo 'tra na muhyanti vidyāvantaś ca ye janāḥ. || 51 ||
yuge yuge bhavanty ete sarve dakṣādayo nṛpaḥ |
punaś caiva nirudhyante, vidvāṃs tatra na muhyati. || 52 ||
jyaiṣṭhyam̃ kāniṣṭhyam̃ apy eṣām̃ pūrvam̃ nāsiḍ dvijottamaḥ̃ |
tapa eva garīyo 'bhūt prabhāvaś caiva kāraṇam̃. || 53 ||
imāṃ visṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya yo vidyāt saccarācarāṃ, |
prajāvān) āyur uttīrṇaḥ) svargaloke mahiyate. || 54 ||
evam) sargaḥ samakhyātaś cākṣuṣasya samāsataḥ |
ity ete ṣaḍ visargā hi ) krāntā manvantarātmakāḥ || 541 ||
svāyambhuvādyāḥ samkṣepāc cākṣuṣāntā yathākramaṃ. |
ete sargā yathā prājñaiḥ) proktā vai) dvijasattamāḥ || 542 ||
vaivasvatavisargeṇa) teṣāṃ jñeyas tu vistaraḥ. |
anyūnā nātiriktās te) sarve sargā vivasvataḥ. || 543 ||
ārogvāyuḥ pramaṇena) dharmataḥ kāmato 'ṛthataḥ |
etān eva guṇān eti, yaḥ paṭhaty) anasūyakāḥ. || 544 ||
vaivasvatasya vakṣyāmi sāṃpratasya mahātmanāḥ |
samāsavyāsataḥ sargaṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 545 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 2. Kapitel

munaya (ṛśaya) ūcuḥ̃: devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca gandharvoragarakṣasāṃ |
utpattiṃ vistareṇaiva mama brahman prakīrtaya! || 1 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca: prajāḥ sṛjeti vyādiṣṭaḥ̃ pūrvam̃ dakṣaḥ̃, svayambhuvā |
yathā sasarja bhūtāni, tathā śṛṇuta bho dvijāḥ̃! || 2 ||
mānasāny eva bhūtāni pūrvam evāsṛjat prabhuḥ̃: |
ṛṣin devān sagandharvān asurdn atha rakṣasdn || 3 ||
yakṣabhūtapiśācāmś ca vayaḥpaśumṛgāms tathā. |
yadāsyā manasā sṛṣṭā na vyavardhanta tāḥ prajāḥ̃, || 4 ||
tadā saṃcintya dharmaṃtmā prajāhetoḥ prajāpatiḥ. |
sa maithunena dharmeṇa sisṛksur vividbāḥ prajā, || 5 ||
asiknīm āvahat patnīṃ viraṇasya prajāpateḥ |
sutāṃ, sutapasā yuktāṃ mahatīṃ lokadbāriṇīṃ. || 6 ||
atha putrasahasrāṇi vairanyāṃ pañca vīryavān |
asiknyāṃ janayāmāsa dakṣa eva prajāpatiḥ. || 7 ||
tāms tu dṛṣtvā mahābhāgan samvivardhayiṣun prajāḥ |
devarṣiḥ priyasamvādo nāradaḥ prābravīd idaṃ || 8 ||
nāśāya vacanaṃ teṣāṃ śāpāyaivātmanas tathā, |
yaṃ kaśyapaḥ sutavaraṃ parameṣṭhī vyajījanat. || 9 ||
dakṣasya vai duhitari | mānasaḥ kaśyapasyāsid dakṣaśāpabhayān muniḥ | dakṣaśāpavaśāt punaḥ |
| tasmāt sa kaśyapasyātha | dviṭiyo mānaso 'bhavat |
pūrvaṃ sa hi samutpanno nāradaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ. || 10 ||
asiknyām atha vairanyāṃ bhūyo devarṣisattamaḥ |
taṃ bhūyo janayāmāsa piteva munipuṃgavaṃ. || 10 ||
tena dakṣasya vai putrā haryaśvā iti viśrutaḥ |
dharmaṛthaṃ nāsitaḥ sarve vidhinā ca na samśayaḥ. || 11 ||
tasyodyatas tadā dakṣo | tasyodyatas tadā dakṣaḥ nāśāyāmitavikramaḥ | kruddho nāśāya vai prabhuḥ |
brahmārṣin purataḥ kṛtrā yācitaḥ parameṣthinā. 12 ||
tato 'bhisamḍhiṃ cakre vai dakṣas tu parameṣthinā: |
kanyāyāṃ nārado mahyaṃ tava putro bhaved iti. || 13 ||
tato dakṣaḥ sutāṃ prādāt priyāṃ vai parameṣṭhine, |
tasmāt sa nārado jajñe bhūyaḥ śapabhayād ṛṣiḥ. || 14 ||
munaya (ṛṣaya) ūcuḥ: kathaṃ vināsītāḥ putrā nāradena maharṣiṇā |
prajāpatisutās te vai śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ. || 15 ||
lomaharṣaṇa (sūta) uvāca: dakṣasya putrā haryaśvā vivardhayiṣavaḥ prajāḥ |
samāgatā mahāviryā, nāradas tān uvāca ha: || 16 ||
bāliśā bata yūyaṃ vai, nāsyā jānītha vai bhuvaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ sraṣṭukāmā vai prajāḥ prācetasātmajāḥ, || 17 ||
antar ūrdhvam adhaś caiva kathaṃ sraṣyatha vai prajāḥ? |
te tu tadvacanaṃ śrutvā prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ; || 18 ||
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. |
haryaśveṣv atha naṣṭeṣu dakṣaḥ prācetasāḥ punaḥ || 19 ||
vairanyāṃ atha putrāṇāṃ sahasram asṛjat prabhuḥ. |
vivardhayiṣavas te tu śabalāśvās tathā prajāḥ || 20 ||
prajā vivardhayiṣavaḥ śabalāśvāḥ punas tu te || 20 ||
pūrvoktaṃ vacanaṃ te tu nāradena pracoditāḥ. |
anyonyam ūcus te sarve: samyag āha mahān ṛsiḥ; || 21 ||
bhrātṛṇāṃ padavī caiva gantavyā nātra samśayaḥ! |
jñātvā pramaṇaṃ pṛthvyāś ca sukhamaṃ srakṣyāmahe prajāḥ. || 22 ||
| [ekāgrāḥ svasthamanasā yathāvad anuśāsitāḥ ] te 'pi tenaiva mārgeṇa prayātāḥ sarvatodiśaṃ, |
adyāpi na nivartante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ. || 23 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai bhrātā bhrātur anveṣaṇe rataḥ |
prayāto naśyati kṣipraṃ, tan na kāryaṃ vipaścitā || 24 ||
naṣṭeṣu śabalāśveṣu dakṣaḥ kruddho 'śapad vibhuḥ |
nāradam: nāśam ehīti garbhavāsaṃ vaseti ca! || 25 ||
tamś caiva naṣṭān vijñāya putrān dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ |
saṣṭiṃ dakṣo 'sṛjat kanya vairanyāṃ, iti naḥ śrutaṃ. || 26 ||
tās tadā pratijagrāha bhāryārthaṃ kaśyapaḥ prabhuḥ |
dharmaḥ somaś ca bhagavāms tathaiṣvānye maharṣayaḥ. || 27 ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya, kaśyapāya trayodaśa, |
saptaviṃśati somāya, catasro 'riṣṭanemine, || 28 ||
dve caiva bahuputrāya, dve caiv-aṅgirase tathā, |
dve kṛśāśvāya viduṣe, tāsāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu: || 29 ||
| imāṃ viśṛṣṭiṃ dakṣasya kṛtsnāṃ yo veda tattvataḥ, |
| āyuṣmān kīrtimān dhanyaḥ prajāvāms ca bhavaty uta. || 29 ||
arundhatī vasur yāmī lambā bhānur marutvatī |
saṃkalpā ca muhūrtā ca sādhyā viśvā tathaiva ca, || 30 ||
dharmapatnyo daśa tv etās, tās apatyāni bodhata: |
viśvedevās tu viśvāyāḥ, sādhyā sādhyān vyajāyata, || 31 ||
marutvatyā marutvanto, vasos tu vasavaḥ sutāḥ, |
bhānos tu bhānavaḥ putra, muhūrtās tu muhūrtajāḥ, || 32 ||
lambāyās caiva ghoṣo 'tha, nāgavīthī ca yāmijā, |
pṛthiviviṣayaṃ sarvam arundhatyāṃ vyajāyata, || 33 ||
samkalpāyās tu sarvātmā jajñe samkalpa eva hi. |
nāgavīthyāṃ ca yāminyāṃ vṛśalās ca vyajāyata. || 34 ||
parā yāḥ somapatnīs ca dakṣaḥ prācetaso dadau, |
sarvā nakṣatranāmnayas tā jyotiṣe parikīrtitāḥ. || 34 ||
ye tv anye khyātimanto vai devā jyotiḥpurogamāḥ, |
vasavo 'ṣṭau samākhyaṭās, teṣāṃ vakṣyāmi vistaram: || 35 ||
āpo dhruvaś ca somas ca dharaś caiv-ānilo 'nalaḥ |
pratyūṣaś ca prabhāsaś ca vasavo nāmabhiḥ smṛtāḥ || 36 ||
apasya putro vaitaṇḍyaḥ śramaḥ śānto munis tathā; |
dhruvasya putro bhagavān kālo lokaprakālanaḥ. || 37 ||
somasya bhagavān varcā, varcasvi yena jāyate; |
dharasya putro draviṇo hutahavyavahas tathā. || 38 ||
manoharāyaḥ śīśiraḥ prāṇo 'tha ramaṇas tathā. |
anilasya śivā bhāryā, tasyāḥ putro manojavāḥ || 39 ||
avijñātagatiś caiva dvau putrāv anilasya ca. |
agniputraḥ kumāras tu śarastambhe vyajāyata, || 40 ||
tasya śakho viśakhaś ca naigameyaś ca pṛṣṭhataḥ, |
apatyaṃ kṛttikānāṃ tu kārttikeya iti smṛtaḥ. || 41 ||
skandaḥ sanatkumāraś ca sṛṣṭaḥ pādena tejasaḥ. |
pratyūṣasya viduḥ putraṃ ṛṣiṃ nāmnaṭha devalaṃ. || 42 ||
dvau putrau devalasyāpi kṣamāvantau manīṣinau; |
bṛhaspates tu bhaginiḥ varastrī brahmacārīḥ || 43 ||
yogasiddhā jagat kṛtsnam asaktā vicacāra ha; |
prabhāsasya tu sā bhāryā vasūnām aṣṭamasya ca, || 44 ||
viśvakarmā mahābhāgo yasāṃ jajñe prajāpatiḥ, |
kartā śilpasahasrānāṃ tridaśānāṃ ca vardhakiḥ, || 45 ||
bhūṣaṇānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kartā, śilpavatāṃ varaḥ, |
yaḥ sarveṣāṃ vimānāni daivatānāṃ cakāra ha, || 46 ||
manuṣyāś copajīvanti yasya śilpaḥ mahātmanaḥ. |
surabhi kāṣyapād rudrān ekādaśa vinirmame || 47 ||
| mahādevaprasādena, tapasā bhāvitā satī: |
ajaikapād ahirbudhnyas tvaṣṭā rudraś ca vīryavān. || 48 ||
tvaṣṭuś caivatmajaḥ śrimān) viśvarūpo mahāyaśāḥ) |
haraś ca bahurūpaś ca tṛyambakaś cāparājitāḥ || 49 ||
vṛṣākapiś ca śambhuś ca kapardī raivatas tathā |
mṛgavyādhaś ca śarvaś) ca kapālī ca dvijottamāḥ). || 50 ||
ekādaśaite kathita) rudrās tribhuvaneśvarāḥ. |
śataḥ tv evaḥ samākhyātaḥ rudrāṇām amitaujasām). || 51 ||
| purāṇe muniśārdūlā) yair vyāptaṃ sacarācaram). |
| dārān śṛṇudhvaṃ viprendrāḥ kaśyapasya prajāpateḥ)! || 51 ||
aditir ditir danuś caiva) ariṣṭā surasā khaśā) |
surabhir vinatā caiva tāmra krodhavaśā irā) || 52 ||
kadrūr) muniś) ca bho viprās), tās) apatyāni bodhata)! |
pūrvamanvanṭare śreṣṭhā) dvādaśāsan surottamāḥ || 53 ||
tuṣitā nāma, te 'nyonyan ūcur vaivasvate 'ntare |
upasthite 'tiyaśasaś) cākṣuṣasyāntare) manoḥ: || 54 ||
hitārthaṃ sarvalokānām) samāgamya parasparaṃ |
āgacchata drutaḥ devā), aditiḥ sampraviṣya vai) 55 ||
manvantare prasūyāmas, tan naḥ) śreyo bhaviṣyati). |
evam uktvā) tu te sarve cākṣuṣasyāntare manoḥ || 56 ||
mārīcāt kaśyapāj jātās tv adityā dakṣakanyayā. |
tatra viṣṇuś ca śakraś ca jajñāte punar eva hi, || 57 ||
aryamā caiva dhātā ca tvaṣṭā puṣā tathaiva ca |
vivasvān savitā caiva mitro varuṇa eva ca || 58 ||
amṣo bhagaś cātitejā ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam āsan ye tuṣitāḥ surāḥ, || 59 ||
vaivasvate 'n'tare te vai ādityā dvādaśa smṛtāḥ. |
saptaviṃśati yāḥ proktāḥ somapatnyo mahāvrataḥ, || 60 ||
tāsām apatyāny abhavan dīptāny amitatejasaḥ. |
ariṣṭanemipatnīnām apatyānīha ṣoḍaśa: || 61 ||
bahuputrasya viduṣaś catasro vidyutāḥ smṛtāḥ, |
prātyaṅgirasajāḥ śreṣṭhāṛco brahmārṣisatkṛtāḥ. || 62 ||
kṛṣāśvasya tu devarṣer devapraharaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ, |
ete yugasahasrānte jāyante punar eva hi. || 63 ||
sarve devagaṇāś cātra trayastrimṣat tu kāmajāḥ, |
teṣām api ca bho viprā nirodhotpattir ucyate. || 64 ||
yathā sūryasya gagana udayāstamayāv iba, |
evaṃ devanikāyās te saṃbhavanti yuge yuge. || 65 ||
dityāḥ putradvayaṃ jajñe kaśyapād, iti naḥ śrutam: |
hiraṇyakaśipuś caiva hiraṇyākṣaś ca vīryavān, || 66 ||
siṃhikā cābhavat kanyā, vipracitteḥ parigrahaḥ, |
| saimhikeyā iti khyātā yasyāḥ putrā mahābalāḥ. || 67 ||
hiraṇyakaśipoḥ putrāś catvāraḥ prathitaujasaḥ: |
anuhrādaś ca hrādaś ca prahrādaś caiva vīryavān, || 68 ||
saṃhrādaś ca caturtho 'bhūd. hrādaputro hradas tathā, |
saṃhrādaputraḥ sundaś ca nisundaś caiva tāv ubhau. |
hradasya putra āyuṣmān śibir bāskala eva ca. || 69 ||
virocanaś ca prāhrādir, balir jajñe virocanāt. |
baleḥ putraśataṃ tv āsid bānajyeṣṭhaṃ tapodhanāḥ: || 70 ||
dhṛtarāṣṭraś ca sūryaś ca candramāś c-endratāpanaḥ |
kumbhanābho gardabhākṣaḥ kukuṣir, ityevamādayaḥ. || 71 ||
bāṇas teṣām atibalo jyeṣṭhaḥ paśupateḥ priyaḥ. |
purākalpe tu bāṇena prasādy-omāpatiṃ prabhūṃ |
pāṛśvato viharisyāmi, ity evaṃ yācito varāḥ. || 71 ||
hiraṇyākṣasutāḥ pañca vidvāmsaḥ su mahābalāḥ: |
utkaraḥ śakuniś caiva bhūtasamṭāpanas tathā || 72 ||
mahānābhas ca vikrāntaḥ kālanābhas tathaiva ca. |
abhavan danuputrās ca śataṃ tīvraparākrāmāḥ || 73 ||
tapasvino mahāvīryāḥ, prādhānyena bravīmi tān: |
dvimūrdhā śāṅkuniś caiva tathā śāṅkuśirāḥ prabhuḥ || 74 ||
śāṅkukarṇo virādhas ca gaveṣṭhī dundubhis tathā, |
ayomukhaḥ śambaras ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, || 75 ||
māricir maghavāṃś caiva irā gargaśirā vṛkaḥ, |
vikṣobhaṇas ca ketuś ca ketuvīrya-śatahradau, || 76 ||
indrajit sarvajic caiva vajranābhas tathaiva ca, |
ekacakro mahābāhus tārakas ca mahābalāḥ, || 77 ||
vaiśvānaraḥ pulomā ca vidrāvaṇa-mahāśirāḥ, |
svarbhānur vṛṣaparvā ca tuhunḍas ca mahāsurāḥ, || 78 ||
sūksmaś caiva nicandraś ca ūrṇanābho mahāgirīḥ, |
asilomā sukeśas ca śatthas ca balako madaḥ, || 79 ||
tathā gaganamūrdhā ca kumbhanābho mahasuraḥ, |
pramado mayas ca kupatho hayagrīvas ca vīryavān, || 80 ||
vaisṛpaḥ sa-virūpāksaḥ supatho 'tha harāharau, |
hiranyakaśipuś caiva śatamāyas ca śambaraḥ, || 81 ||
śarabbaḥ śalabhas caiva vipracittis ca vīryavān; |
sarva ete danoḥ putraḥ kaśyapād abhijajñire. || 82 ||
| vipracittipradhānās te dānavāḥ sumahābalāḥ, |
eteṣāṃ yad apatyaṃ tu, na tac chakyaṃ dvijottamāḥ |
prasamkhyātuṃ bahutvāc ca putrapautram anantakaṃ. || 82 ||
svarbhānos tu prabhā kanyā, pulomnas tu śacī sutā |
upadānavī hayaśirāḥ, śarmišthā vārṣaparvaṇī. || 83 ||
pulomā kālikā caiva vaiśvānarasute ubhe, |
bahvapatye mahāvīrye mārices tu parigrahaḥ. || 84 ||
tayoḥ putrasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭir dānavanandanaḥ. |
caturdaśa śatāny anyān hiranyapuravāsinaḥ || 85 ||
māricir janayāmāsa mahatā tapasānvitaḥ. |
paulomāḥ kālakeyās ca dānavās te mahābalāḥ. || 86 ||
avadhyā devatānāṃ hi hiraṇyapuravāsinaḥ |
pitāmahaprasādena, ye hatāḥ savyasācinā. || 87 ||
prabbāyā nahuṣaḥ putro, jayantaś ca śacīsutaḥ |
puruṃ jajñe 'tha śarmiṣṭhā, duṣmantam upadānavī. || 88 ||
tato 'pare mahāvīryā dānavās tv atidārunāḥ |
siṃhikāyām athotpannā vipracitteḥ sutās tathā, || 89 ||
daitya-dānavasamyogāj jātās tivraparākramāḥ |
saimhikeyā iti khyātās trayodaśa mahābalāḥ: || 90 ||
vyamśaḥ śalyaś ca balinau nalaś caiva mahābalaḥ |
vātāpir namuciś caiva ilvalaḥ svasṛmas tathā, || 91 ||
añjiko narakaś caiva kālanābhas tathaiva ca, |
rāhur jyeṣṭhas tu tesāṃ vai sūryacandrapramardanaḥ, |
saramānas tathā caiva svarakalpaś ca vīryavān, || 92 ||
mūkaś caiva tuhunḍaś ca hradaputrau babhūvatuḥ, |
mārīcaḥ sundaputraś ca tāḍakāyāṃ vyajāyata, || 93 ||
ete vai dānavāḥ śreṣṭhā danuvaṃśavivardhanāḥ; |
teṣāṃ putraś ca pautraś ca śataṣo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 94 ||
saṃhrādasya tu daityasya nivātakavacāḥ kule |
samutpannāḥ sumahatā tapasā bhāvitātmanaḥ. || 95 ||
tisraḥ koṭyāḥ sutās teṣāṃ maṇivatyaṃ nivāsināḥ, |
avadhyās te 'pi devānām arjunena nipātitāḥ. || 96 ||
ṣaṭ sutāḥ sumaḥāsattvās tāmrāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ: |
kakī śyeni ca bhāsi ca sugrīvi śucir gṛdhrikā. || 97 ||
kakī kākān ajanayad, ulūkapratyulūkakān, |
śyeni śyenāms tathā bhāsi bhāsān gṛdhrāms ca gṛdhṛy api, || 98 ||
śucir audakān pakṣigaṇān, sugrīvi tu vyajāyata |
aśvān uṣṭrān gardabhāms ca. tāmrāvamśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. || 99 ||
vinatāyās tu putrau dvau aruno garuḍas tathā. |
suparṇāḥ patatāṃ śreṣṭho, dāruṇāḥ svena karmaṇā. || 100 ||
surasāyāḥ sahasraṃ tu sarpāṇām amitaujasāṃ |
anekaśirasāṃ teṣāṃ khocarāṇāṃ mahātmanāṃ. || 101 ||
kādraveyās tu balināḥ sahasraṃ amitaujasāḥ |
suparṇavaśagā nāgā jajñire 'neṣkamastakāḥ, || 102 ||
yeṣāṃ pradhānāḥ satatam: śeṣa-vāsuki-takṣakāḥ, |
airavato mahāpadmaḥ kambal-āśvatarāḥ ubhau || 103 ||
elāpatras tathā padmaḥ karkoṭaka-dhanamjayau, |
mahānīla-mahākarnau dhṛtarāṣṭra-balāhakau, || 104 ||
kuḍarāḥ puṣpadamṣṭraś ca durmukhaḥ sumukhas tathā, |
| śāṅkhaś ca śāṅkhaṭalāś ca kapilo vāmanas tathā, |
nahuśaḥ śāṅkharomā ca manir ityevamādayaḥ. || 105 ||
| teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca garuḍena nipātitāḥ |
| caturdaśa sahasraṇi krūrāṇām anilāsināṃ. || 105 ||
gaṇaṃ krodhavaśaṃ vidḍhi, tasya sarve ca daṃṣṭriṇaḥ, |
sthalajāḥ pakṣiṇo 'bjāś ca dharayāḥ prasavāḥ smṛtāḥ. || 106 ||
gāṣ tu vai janayāmāsa surabhir mahiṣāṃś tathā, |
irā vṛkṣalatāvallis tṛṇajātīś ca sarvaśaḥ. || 107 ||
khaśā tu yakṣarakṣāṃsi, munir apsarasas tathā, |
ariṣṭā tu mahāsatvān gandharvān samajījanat. || 108 ||
ete kaśyapadāyādāḥ kiritāḥ sthānujamgamāḥ, |
teṣāṃ putrāś ca pautrāś ca śataśo 'tha sahasraśaḥ. || 109 ||
eṣa manvantare vipraḥ sargaḥ svārocise smṛtaḥ. |
vaivasvate tu mahati vāruṇe vitate kratau || 110 ||
juhvanasya brahmaṇo vai prajāsarga ihocyate. |
pūrvaṃ yatra tu brahmarṣin utpannān sapta mānasān || 111 ||
putratve kalpayāmāsa svayam eva pitamahaḥ, |
tato virodhe devānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca bho dvijaḥ. || 112 ||
ditir vinaṣṭaputrā vai toṣayāmāsa kaśyapaṃ. |
tāṃ kaśyapaḥ prasannātmā, samyag ārādhitas tayā, || 113 ||
vareṇa chandayāmāsa, sā ca vavre varaṃ tadā |
putram indravadhārthāya samartham amitaujasaṃ. || 114 ||
sa ca tasyai varaṃ prādāt prārthitaḥ sumahātapāḥ. dattvā ca varam avyagro mārīcaḥ tām abhāṣata: || 115 ||
indraṃ putro nihantā te, garbhaṃ vai śaradāṃ śataṃ |
yadi dhārayase, śaucatatparā vratam āsthita. || 116 ||
tathety abhihito bhartā tayā devyā mahātapāḥ. |
dhārayāmāsa garbhaṃ tu śuciḥ sā varavarṇinī. || 117 ||
tato 'bhyupāgamad dityāṃ | tatas tv ādhāya so dityāṃ garbham ādhāya kaśyapaḥ | garbhaṃ tāṃ samśitavrataḥ |
rocay'an vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ devānām amitaujasāṃ. || 118 ||
tejaḥ samhṛtya durdharsaṃ avadhyam amarair api |
jagāma parvatāyaiva tapase saṃśitavrataḥ || 118 ||
tasyāś caivāntaraprepsur abhavat pākaśāsanaḥ, |
ūne varṣaśate cāsyā dadarśāntaram acyutaḥ. || 119 ||
akṛtvā pādayoḥ śaucaṃ ditiḥ śayanam āviśat |
nidrāṃ cāhārayāmāsa; tasyāḥ kuksīṃ praviṣya saḥ || 120 ||
vajrapāṇis tato garbhaṃ saptadhā taṃ nyakṛntata |
sa pātyamāno garbho 'tha vajreṇa praruroda ha, || 121 ||
mā rodīr iti taṃ śakraḥ punaḥ punar athābravīt. |
so 'bhavat saptadhā garbhas, tam indro ruṣitaḥ punaḥ || 122 ||
ekaikaṃ saptadhā cakre vajreṇaivārikarṣaṇaḥ. |
maruto nāma te devā babhūvuḥ sumahābalāḥ. || 123 ||
yathoktaṃ vai maghavatā, tathaiva maruto 'bhavan, |
devāś caikonapañcāsat saḥāyā vajrapāṇinaḥ. || 124 ||
teṣām evaṃ pravṛddhānāṃ |
bhūtānāṃ dvijasattamāḥ |
rocayan vai gaṇaśreṣṭhaṃ devānām amitaujasāṃ, || 124 ||
nikāyeṣu nikāyeṣu hariḥ prādāt prajāpatiḥ |
kramaśas tāni rājyāni pṛthupūrvāni bho dvijāḥ. || 125 ||
sa hariḥ puruṣo vīraḥ kṛṣṇo jiṣṇuḥ prajāpatiḥ |
parjanyaṣ tapano vyaktas tasya sarvam idaṃ jagat. || 126 ||
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyag jānato dvijasattamāḥ |
marutāṃ ca śubhaṃ janma śṛṇvataḥ paṭhato 'pi ca |
nāvṛttibhayam astīha, paralokabhayaṃ kutaḥ. || 127 ||
teṣām eva pravṛddhānāṃ hariḥ prādāt prajāpatiḥ |
bhūtasargam imaṃ samyak kathitaṃ śṛṇu cāgrataḥ || 127 ||

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 3. Kapitel  (A. 19.22—29a; Bd. II. 8.1—26a; Br. 4.1—18; H. 257—282; Śidh. 54.95—5512; Vā. 70.1—21; Vi. I. 22.1—13a).

| evaṃ prajāsu sṛṣṭāsu kaśyapena mahātmanā |
| pratiṣṭhitāsu sarvāsu sthāvarāsu carāsu ca || 1 ||
abhiṣicyādhirājye tu pṛṭhuṃ vainyaṃ pitāmahaḥ |
tataḥ kramaṇa rājyaṇi vyādeṣṭum upacakrame. || 1 ||
dvijānāṃ vīrudhāṃ caiva nakṣatragrahayos tathā |
yajñānāṃ tapasāṃ caiva somaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat. || 2 ||
apāṃ tu varuṇaṃ rājye rājñāṃ vaiśravaṇaṃ prabhuṃ, |
bṛhaspatiṃ tu viśveṣāṃ dadāv aṅgirasāṃ patiṃ. || 3 ||
bhṛgūnām adhipaṃ caiva kāvyaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat, |
ādityānāṃ tathā viṣṇuṃ, vasūnām atha pāvakaṃ, || 4 ||
prajāpatināṃ dakṣaṃ tu, marutām atha vāsavaṃ, |
daityānāṃ dānavānāṃ ca prahlādam amitaujasaṃ. || 5 ||
nārāyaṇaṃ tu sādhyānāṃ rudrānāṃ vṛṣabhadhvajaṃ |
vipracittiṃ ca rājānaṃ dānavānām athadiśat. || 6 ||
vaivasvataṃ pitṛnāṃ ca yamaṃ rājye 'bhyāṣecayat |
yāksāṇāṃ rāksasānāṃ ca pārthivānāṃ tathaiva ca || 7 ||
sarvabhūtapiśācānāṃ girīśaṃ śūlapāṇinaṃ, |
śailānāṃ himavantaṃ ca, nadīnām atha sāgaraṃ, || 8 ||
gandhānāṃ marutāṃ caiva, bhutānām aśarirīṇāṃ |
śabdākāśavatāṃ caiva vāyuṃ balavatāṃ varaṃ || 9 ||
sāgarānāṃ nadīnāṃ ca meghānāṃ varṣitasya ca. |
gandharvānām adhipatiṃ cakre citrarathaṃ prabhuṃ, || 10 ||
nāgānāṃ vāsukiṃ cakre, sarpānām atha takṣakaṃ, |
sarveṣāṃ daṃṣtrīnāṃ śeṣaṃ rājānam abhyāṣecayat. || 11 ||
vāraṇānāṃ tu rājānaṃ airāvatam athadiśat, |
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ, garuḍaṃ caiva pakṣiṇāṃ. || 12 ||
uccaiḥśravasam aśvānāṃ rājānaṃ cābhyaṣecayat, |
pakṣiṇām atha sarveṣāṃ garuḍaṃ patatāṃ varaṃ. || 12 ||
mṛgānām atha śārdūlaṃ, govṛṣaṃ tu gavām api |
vanaspatināṃ rājānaṃ plakṣam evābhyaṣecayat. || 13 ||
sarvāpsarogaṇḍanāṃ ca kāmadevaṃ tathā prabhuṃ, |
ṛtūnām atha māsanāṃ divasānāṃ tathaiva ca || 14 ||
pakṣānāṃ ca kṣapānāṃ ca | pakṣānāṃ ca vipakṣānāṃ muhūrtatithiparvaṇāṃ | muhūrtānāṃ ca parvaṇāṃ |
kalākāṣṭhāpramāṇānām ṛtor ayanayos tathā |
gaṇitasyātha yogasya cakre saṃvatsaraṃ prabhuṃ. || 15 ||
evaṃ vibhajya rājyāni krameṇaiva pitāmahaḥ |
diśāṃ pālān atha tataḥ sthāpayāmāsa sarvataḥ: || 15 ||
pūrvasyāṃ diśi putraṃ tu vairājasya prājapateḥ |
diśa pālāṃ sudhanvānaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 16 ||
dakṣiṇasyāṃ diśi tathā kardamasya prājapateḥ |
putraṃ śaṅkhapadaṃ nāma rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 17 ||
paścimasyāṃ diśi tathā rajasaḥ putram acyutaṃ |
ketumantaṃ mahātmānaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 18 ||
tathā hiraṇyaromānaṃ parjanyasya prājapateḥ |
udīcyāṃ diśi durdharṣaṃ rājānaṃ so 'bhyasecayat. || 19 ||
| manuṣyānām adhipatiḥ cakre vaivasvataṃ manuṃ. |
taṃ iyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā saptadvīpā sapattanā |
yathāpradeśam adyāpi dharmeṇa pratipālyate. || 20 ||
| svāyambhuve 'ntare pūrvaṃ brahmanā te 'bhiṣecitāḥ, |
nṛpā hy ete 'bhiṣicyante, manavo ye bhavanti vai || 20 ||
manvantaresv atītesu gatā hy etesu pārthivāḥ. |
evam anye 'bhiṣicyante prāpte manvantare punaḥ. |
atītānāgatāḥ sarve smṛtā manvantareśvarāḥ. || 202 ||
rājasūyābhiṣiktas tu pṛthur ebhir narādhīpaiḥ |
vedadṛṣṭena vidhinā rājarājye narādhīpaḥ. || 21 ||
tato manvantare 'tīte cākṣuṣe 'mitatejasi |
vaivasvatāya manave pṛthivyāṃ rājyam ādiśat. || 211 ||
tasya vistaram ākhyāsye manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhavatāṃ cānukūlyāya, yadi śrotum ihecchatha; |
mahad hy etad adhiṣṭhānaṃ purāṇe pariniṣṭhitaṃ. || 212 ||
[dhanyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ svargavāsakaraṃ śubham.]

## 2. Abschnitt. Vaṃśa | 4. Kapitel  (Bd. I. 36.117—37.22a; Br. 4.19—122; H. 283—405; Śidh. 56.1—57.26;Vā. 62.99—63.21.)

ṛśaya ūcuḥ: vistareṇa pṛṭhor janma kīrtayasva mahāmate, |
yathā mahātmanā tena dugdhā pūrvaṃ vasumḍharā, || 1 ||
yathā ca pitṛbhir dugdhā, yathā devair, mahārśibhiḥ, |
yathā daityaiś ca nāgaiś ca, yathā yakṣair, yathā drumaiḥ, || 2 ||
yathā śailaiḥ piśācais ca gandharvaiś ca dvijottamaiḥ |
rākṣasais ca mahāsattvair, yathā dugdhā vasumḍharā. || 3 ||
teṣāṃ pātraviśeṣāmś ca vaktum arhasi suvrata vatsān kṣīraviśeṣāmś ca dogdhāraṃ cānupūrvaśaḥ, || 4 ||
yasmāc ca kāraṇāt pāṇir venasya mathitaḥ purā |
kruddhair maharśibhiḥ pūrvaiḥ, kāraṇaṃ tac ca kīrtaya || 5 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca: kīrtayisyāmi vo vipraḥ pṛṭhor vainyasya saṃbhavaṃ, |
ekāgrāḥ prayatās caiva śusrūṣadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ. || 6 ||
nāśuceḥ kṣudramanaso | nāśucer nāpi pāpāya nāsiṣyāyāvratāya vā | nāsiṣyāyāhitāya ca |
kīrtayeyam idaṃ viprāḥ | varṇayeyam idaṃ brahma kṛtaghnāyāhitāya vā. || 7 || nāvratāya kathamcana. || 7 ||
svargyaṃ yaśasyam āyuṣyaṃ dhanyaṃ vedaiṣ ca sammitaṃ |
rahasyaṃ ṛṣibhiḥ proktaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ vai yathātathaṃ! || 8 ||
yaṣ cemaṃ kathayen nityaṃ pṛthor vainyasya sambhavaṃ |
brāhmaṇebhyo namaskṛtya, na sa śocet kṛtākṛtaṃ. || 9 ||
āśīd dharmasya goptā vai | goptā dharmasya rājāsau pūrvam atrisamaḥ prabhuḥ | babhūv-atrisamaḥ prabhuḥ |
atrivamśasamutpannas tv aṅgo nāma prajāpatiḥ. || 10 ||
tasya putro 'bhavad veno nātyarthaṃ dharmikas tathā |
jāto mṛtyusutāyāṃ vai sunīthāyāṃ prajāpatiḥ. || 11 ||
sa mātāmahadoṣeṇa venāḥ kālātmajātmajaḥ |
svadharmaṃ pṛsthataḥ kṛtvā kāmāl lobheṣv avartata. || 12 ||
maryādāṃ sthāpayāmāsa dharmāpetāṃ sa pārtihvaḥ, |
vedadharmān atikramya so 'dharmanirato 'bhavat. || 13 ||
ayiṣyāmi vo viprāḥ; br. śṛṇudhvaṃ kīrtayiṣyāmi; h. hanta te niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkārāḥ prajās tasmin praśāsati |
prāvartan, na papuḥ somaṃ hutaṃ yajñesu devatāḥ. || 14 ||
na yaṣṭavyaṃ na hotavyam iti tasya prajāpateḥ |
āsit pratijñā krūreyaṃ vināse pratyupasthite. || 15 ||
aham ijyaś ca pūjyaś ca yajñe devaavijātibhiḥ, |
mayi yajñā vidhātavyā, mayi hotavyam ity api. || 16 ||
tam atikrāntamaryādam ādadānam asamprataṃ |
ūcur maharṣayaḥ sarve marīcipramukhās tadā: || 17 ||
vayaṃ dikṣāṃ prave'kṣyāmaḥ samvatsaragaṇān bahūn, |
adharmaṃ kuru mā vena, eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ. || 18 ||
nidhane 'treḥ prasūtas tvaṃ prajāpatir asamśayaṃ, |
prajās ca pālayiṣye 'ham iti te samayaḥ kṛtaḥ. || 19 ||
tāms tathā bruvataḥ sarvān maharṣin abravīt tadā |
venāḥ prahasya durbuddhir imam artham anarthavit: || 20 ||
sraṣṭā dharmasya kāś cānyaḥ, śrotavyaṃ kasya vā mayā, |
śrutavīryatapaḥsatyair mayā vā kaḥ samo bhuvi? || 21 ||
prabhavaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ dharmānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
saṃmūḍhā na vidur nūnaṃ |
bhavanto māṃ acetasaḥ. || 22 ||
icchan daheyaṃ pṛthivīṃ, plā vayeyaṃ jalais tathā |
dyāṃ bhuvaṃ caiva rundhreyaṃ, nātra kāryā vicāraṇā. ||
yadā na śakyate mohād avalepāc ca pārthivaḥ |
anunetuṃ tadā venas, tataḥ kruddhā maharṣayaḥ. || 24 ||
nigṛhya taṃ mahātmāno visphurantaṃ mahābalaṃ |
tato 'sya savyam ūruṃ te mamanthur jātamanyavaḥ. || 25 ||
tasmin nimathyamāne vai rājña ūrau tu jajñivān |
brasvo 'timātraṃ puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇaś cāpi babhūva ha. || 26 ||
sa bhitaḥ prāñjalir bhūtvā tasthivān vyākulendriyaḥ. |
tam atrir vihvalaṃ dṛṣtvā niṣidety abravīt tadā. || 27 ||
niṣādavamśakartāsau babhūvānantavikramaḥ, |
dhīvarān asṛjac cāpi venakalmaṣasambhavān. || 28 ||
ye cānye vindhyaniḷayās tukhārās tumburās tathā |
adharmarucayaś cāpi, vidhhi tān venakalmaṣān. || 29 ||
tataḥ punar mahātmānaḥ pāṇiṃ venasya dakṣiṇaṃ |
araṇim iva samrabdhā mamanthur jātamanyavaḥ. || 30 ||
pṛṭhus tasmāt samutpannaḥ karāj jvalanasamṇibhaḥ |
| pṛṭhoḥ karatalād vāpi yasmāj jātaḥ, pṛṭhus tataḥ. |
dīpyamānaḥ svavapuṣā sākṣād agnir iva jvalan || 31 ||
| [sa dhanvī kavacī jātaḥ pṛṭhur eva mahāyaśāḥ | ]
ādyam ājagavaṃ nāma dhanur gṛhya mahāravaṃ |
śarāṃś ca divyān raksārthaṃ kavacaṃ ca mahāprabhaṃ. || 32 ||
tasmin jāte 'tha bhūtāni samprahṛṣṭāni sarvaśāḥ |
samāpetur mahārajāṃ, venāś ca tridivaṃ yayau. || 33 ||
samutpannena rājarśiḥ satputreṇa mahātmanā |
trātaḥ sa puruṣavyāghraḥ puṇnāmno narakāt tadā. || 34 ||
taṃ samudrās ca nadyas ca ratnāny ādāya sarvaśaḥ |
toyāni cābhiṣekārthaṃ sarva evopatasthire. || 35 ||
pitāmahas ca bhagavān devair āngirasaiḥ saha |
sthāvarāṇi ca bhūtāni jaṅgamāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 36 ||
samāgamya tadā vainyam abhyasiñcan narādhīpaṃ |
mahatā rājarājyena prajāpālaṃ mahādyutiṃ. || 37 ||
sobhiṣikto mahātejā | so 'bhiṣikto mahārājo vidhivad dharmakovidaiḥ | devair aṅgirasaiḥ sutaiḥ |
ādhirājye tadā rājñaṃ pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān. || 38 ||
pitṛāparañjitās tasya prajās tenānurañjitāḥ; |
anurāgāt tatas tasya | tato rājeti nāmāsyā nāma rājety ajāyata || 39 || a'nurāgād ajāyata. || 39 ||
āpas tastambhire tasya samudram abhiyāsyataḥ, |
parvatās ca dadur mārgaṃ, dhvajabhañgaś ca nābhavat. || 40 ||
akṛṣṭapacyā pṛthivī, sidhyanty annāni cintayā, |
sarvakāmadughā gāvaḥ, puṭake puṭake madhu. || 41 ||
etasminn eva kāle tu yajñe paitāmahe śubhe |
sūtaḥ sūtyāṃ samutpannaḥ sautye 'hani mahāmatiḥ. || 42 ||
tasminn eva mahāyajñe jajñe prājño 'tha māgadhaḥ. |
sāmageṣu ca gāyatsu srugbhāṇḍe vaiśvadevake |
sāmagāne samutpannas, tasmān māgadha ucyate. || 42 ||
aindrena haviṣā cāpi haviḥ pṛktaṃ bṛhaspateḥ |
juhāv-endrāya devena, tataḥ sūto vyajāyata. || 42 ||
pramādas tatra samjajñe prāyaścittaṃ ca karmasu |
śiṣyahavyena yat pṛktam abhibhūtaṃ guror haviḥ || 42 ||
adharottaracāreṇa jajñe tadvarṇavaikṛtaṃ. |
yac ca kṣatrāt samabhadv brāhmaṇyaṃ hīnayonitaḥ, || 42 ||
sūtaḥ pūrveṇa sādharmaṣāt tulvadharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ; |
madhyamo hy eṣa sūtasya dharmāḥ kṣatropajīvanaṃ, || 42 ||
rathanāgāsvacaritaṃ jaghanyaṃ ca cikitsitaṃ. |
pṛthoḥ stavārthaṃ tau tatra samāhūtau maharṣibhiḥ. || 43 ||
tāv ūcur ṛṣayaḥ sarve: stūyatām eṣa pārthivaḥ, |
karmaitad anurūpaṃ vāṃ, pātraṃ cāyaṃ narādhipaḥ. || 44 ||
tāv ūcatus tadā sarvāṃs tān ṛṣīn sūtamāgadhaḥ: |
āvāṃ devān ṛṣīṃś caiva prīṇayāvaḥ svakarmabhiḥ. || 45 ||
na cāsyā vidvo vai karma na tathālakṣaṇaṃ yaśaḥ, |
stotraṃ yenāsyā kuryāva rājñas tejasvino dvijāḥ. || 46 ||
ṛṣibhis tau niyuktau tu: bhaviṣyaiḥ stūyatām iti, |
dānadharmarato nityaṃ satyavāk samjītendriyaḥ || 461 ||
jñānaśilo vadānyaś ca) samgrāmeṣv aparājitaḥ |
yāni karmāni kṛtavān pṛthuḥ paścān) mahābalaḥ. || 47 ||
satyavāg dānaśilo 'yaṃ satyasamḍho nareśvaraḥ |
śrimān jaitraḥ kṣamāśilo vikrānto dṛṣṭāśāsanaḥ || 471 ||
dharmajñāś ca kṛtajñāś ca dayāvān priyabhāṣaṇaḥ |
mānyamānayitā yajvā brahmanyaḥ satyasamḍgaraḥ |
śamaḥ śantaḥ ca nirato vyavahārasthito nṛpaḥ. || 472 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai loke) staveṣu munisattamāḥ) |
āśirvādāḥ prayujyante sūtamāgadhabandibhiḥ). || 48 ||
tayoḥ) stavānte supṛitaḥ pṛthuḥ prādāt prajeśvaraḥ |
anūpadeśaṃ sūtāya magadham) māgadbhāya ca. || 49 ||
taṃ dṛṣtvā paramaprītāḥ prajāḥ) prāhur) maharṣayaḥ): |
vṛttinām) eṣa vo dātā bhaviṣyati narādhīpaḥ). || 50 ||
tato vainyaṃ mahābhāgaṃ) prajāḥ samabbidudruvuḥ: |
tvam) no vṛttiṃ vidhatsveti maharṣivacanāt tadā). || 51 ||
so 'bhidrutaḥ prajābhis tu prajāhitacikiṣayā |
dhanur gṛhya pṛsatkāṃś ca pṛthivim ārdayad balī || 52 ||
tato vainyabbayatrastā gaur bhūtvā prādravan mahī, tāṃ pṛthur dhanur ādāya dravantim anvadhāvata. || 53 ||
sā lokān brahmālokādīn gatvā vainyabhayāt tadā |
pradadarśāgrato vainyaṃ pragṛhitaśarāsanaṃ || 54 ||
jvaladbbhir niśitair bāṇair dīptatejasam acyutaṃ |
mahāyogaṃ mahātmānaṃ durdharṣaṃ amarair api. || 55 ||
alabbhanti tu sā trāṇaṃ vainyam evānvapadyata |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā pūjyā lokais tribhiḥ sadā || 56 ||
uvāca vainyam: nādharmaṃ strīvadhe paripaśyasi? |
kathaṃ dhārayitā cāsi prajā rājan mayā vinā? || 57 ||
mayi lokāḥ sthitā rājan, mayedaṃ dhāryate jagat, |
mad ṛte ca vinaśyeyuḥ prajāḥ pārthiva, viddhi tat! || 58 ||
na mām arhasi hantuṃ vai, śreyaś cet tvaṃ cikīrṣasi |
prajānāṃ, pṛthivīpāla, śṛṇu cedaṃ vaco mama! || 59 ||
upāyataḥ samārabdhāḥ sarve sidhyanty upakramāḥ; |
| upāyaṃ paśya, yena tvaṃ dhārayethāḥ prajā nṛpa. || 60 ||
hatvapi māṃ na śaktas tvaṃ prajānāṃ pālāne nṛpa; |
anubhūta bhaviṣyāmi, yaccha kopaṃ mahādyute! || 61 ||
avadhyāś ca striyaḥ prāhus tiryagyonigatesv api |
sattvesu, pṛthivīpāla, na dharmaṃ tyaktum arhasi. || 62 ||
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ vākyaṃ śrutvā rājā mahāmanāḥ |
kopaṃ nigṛhya dharmātmā vasudhām idam abravīt: || 63 ||
ekasyārthāya yo hanyād ātmano vā parasya vā |
bahūn vā prāṇino 'thaikam, | ekaṃ prāṇaṃ bahūn vāpi, bhavet tasyeha pātakaṃ, || 64 || karma tasyāsti pātakaṃ. || 64 ||
sukham edhanti bahavo | yasmimś tu nihate bhadre yasmimś tu nihate 'śubhe, | edhante bahavaḥ sukhaṃ |
tasmin hate nāsti śubhe pātakaṃ copapātakaṃ. || 65 ||
[ekasmin yatra nidhanaṃ prāpite duṣṭakārīni |
bahūnāṃ bhavati kṣemaṃ, tatra puṇyaprado vadhaḥ. ||]
so 'haṃ prajānimittaṃ tvāṃ haniṣyāmi vasumdhare, |
yadi me vacanaṃ nādyā kariṣyasi jagaddhitaṃ. || 66 ||
tvāṃ nihatyādyā bāṇena macchāsanaparānmukhīṃ |
ātmānaṃ prathayitvāhaṃ prajā dhārayitā svayaṃ. || 67 ||
sā tvam̃ śāsanam āsthāya mama dharmabhṛtāṃ vare |
samjīvaya prajāḥ sarvāḥ, | samjīvaya prajā nityaṃ, samarthā hy asi dhāraṇe. || 68 || śaktā hy asi na samśayaḥ. || 68 ||
duhitṛtvam̃ ca me gaccha, tata enam aham̃ śaraṃ |
niyaccheyam̃ tvadvadhārtham udyantam̃ ghoradarśanaṃ. || 69 ||
| pratyuvāca tato vainyam evam uktā satī mahī: |
sarvam etad aham̃ vīra vidhāsyāmi na samśayaḥ. || 70 ||
| [upāyataḥ̃ samārabdhāḥ̃ sarve sidhyanty upakramāḥ̃; |
upāyam̃ paśya, yena tvam̃ dhārayethāḥ̃ prajā imāḥ̃. || ]
vatsam̃ tu mama tam̃ paśya, kṣareyam̃ yena vatsalā, |
samām̃ ca kuru sarvatra mām̃ tvam̃ dharmabhṛtām̃ vara, || 71 ||
yathā viṣyandamānam̃ me kṣīram̃ sarvatra bhāvayet. |
tata utsārayāmāsa śailāñ śatasahasraśaḥ̃ || 72 ||
dhanuṣkoṭyā tadā vainyas, tena śailā vivardhitāḥ̃. |
| [pṛthur vainyas tadā cakre mahīṃ rājā samām̃ tataḥ̃. | ]
manvantareṣv atīteṣu viṣamāṣid vasum̃dharā, || 73 ||
svabhāvenāblavam̃s tasyāḥ̃ samāni viṣamāṇi ca. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare pūrvam̃ āṣid etat purā kila. || 74 ||
na hi pūrvavisarge vai viṣame) pṛthivitale |
pravibhāgaḥ purāṇaṃ vā) grāmaṇāṃ vābhavat tadā). || 75 ||
na sasyāni na goraksā) na kṛṣir na vanikpathaḥ |
| naiva satyānṛtaṃ cāsin) na lobho na ca matsaraḥ. || 76 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare tasmin) sāmpratam) samupasthite) |
vainyāt prabhṛti loke 'smin) sarvasyaitasya saṃbhavaḥ. || 77 ||
yatra yatra samaṃ tv asyā) bhūmer āsit tadā) dvijā), |
tatra tatra prajāḥ sarvā nivāsaṃ samarocayan. || 78 ||
āhāraḥ) phalamūlani) prajānāṃ kṛcchreṇa mahata yukta), ity evam anuśūrama). || 79 ||
sa) kalpayitvā vatsaṃ tu manuṃ svāyaṃbhuvaṃ prabhuṃ svapāṇau puruṣavyāghro) dudoha pṛthivīṃ tataḥ). || 80 ||
sasyajātāni sarvāni pṛthur) vainyaḥ pratāpavān, |
tenānnena prajāḥ sarvā) vartante 'dyāpi sarvaśāḥ). || 81 ||
ṛṣibhiḥ śrūyate) cāpi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā, |
vatsaḥ somo 'bhavat) teṣām) dogdhā cāpi bṛhaspatiḥ), || 82 ||
pātram āsit tu chandāṃsi) gāyātṛyādīni sarvaśāḥ), |
kṣīram āsit tadā teṣāṃ tapo) brahma) ca śāśvataṃ. || 83 ||
punas tato) devagaṇaiḥ puramḍarapurogamaiḥ) |
kāñcanam) pātram ādāya dugdhéyaṃ śrūyate mahī). || 84 ||
vatsas tu maghavān āsid, dogdhā tu) savitā prabhuḥ), |
kṣīram ūrjaskaraṃ caiva), vartante yena) devatāḥ). || 85 ||
pitṛbhiḥ śrūyate cāpi) punar dugdhā vasumḍharā) |
rājataṃ pātram ādāya svadhām) amitavikramaiḥ). || 86 ||
| [svadhāmṛtaṃ ca pitṛnām āsid dogdh-āryamā tathā, | ]
yamo vaivasvatas teṣām) āsid) vatsaḥ pratāpavān), |
| antakaś cābhavad dogdhā kalo lokaprakalanaḥ). || 87 ||
nāgais ca śrūyate dugdhā vatsaṃ kṛtvā tu takṣakaṃ |
alābuṃ pātram ādāya viṣaṃ kṣīraṃ tadā mahī. || 88 ||
nāgānāṃ vai dvijaśreṣṭha sarpānāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ || 89 ||
asuraiḥ śrūyate cāpi punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
āyasaṃ pātram ādāya māyāṃ śatrūnibarhinīṃ. || 91 ||
virocanas tu prāhrādir vatsas tesām abhūt tadā, |
ṛtvig dvimūrdhā daityānāṃ, madhur dogdhā mahābalaḥ. || 92 ||
tayaite māyayādyāpi sarve māyāvino 'surāḥ |
vartayanty amitaprajñās, tad eṣām amitaṃ balaṃ. || 93 ||
| yakṣais ca śrūyate rājan punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
āmapātre punar dugdhā tv antarḍhānam iyaṃ mahī || 94 ||
tena kṣīreṇa yakṣāṣ ca rākṣasāṣ cāmaropamāḥ |
vartayanti piśācāṣ ca bhūtasanghās tathaiva ca. || 99 ||
padmapātre punar dugdhā gandharvaiḥ sapsarogaṇaiḥ |
vatsaṃ citrarathaṃ kṛtvā śucīn gandhāms tathaiva ca. || 100 ||
teṣāṃ vasuruciṣ tv āsid dogdhā putro muneḥ śuciḥ |
gandharvarājo 'tibalo mahātmā sūryasaṃnibhaḥ. || 101 ||
śailaiṣ ca śrūyate dugdhā punar devī vasumḍharā |
tatrauṣadhīr mūrtimatī ratnāni vividhāni ca. || 102 ||
vatsas tu himavān āsid, dogdhā merur mahāgiriḥ, |
| pātraṃ tu śailam evāsit, tena śailāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ. || 103 ||
| śrūyate vṛkṣavīrudbhiḥ punar dugdhā vasumḍharā |
pālāśaṃ pātram ādāya chinnadagdhaprarohaṇaṃ. || 104 ||
kamadhuk puṣpitaḥ śālāḥ plakṣo vatso yaśasvināṃ, |
sarvakāmadughā dogdhri | sarvakāmadughā dogdhri sarvasasyaprarohaṇi. || 105 || | pṛthivī bhūtabhāvinī. || 105 ||
seyaṃ dhātri vidhātri ca pavanī ca vasumḍharā |
| dugdhā hitārthaṃ lokānāṃ pṛṭhuneti hi naḥ śrutaṃ. |
carācarasya sarvasya pratiṣṭhā yonir eva ca || 106 ||
āsid iyaṃ samudrāntā medinīti pariśrutā. |
| vasu dhārayate yasmād, vasudhā tena cocyate. |
madhu-kaiṭabhayoḥ kṛtsnā medasā saṃpariplutā, || 107 ||
teneyaṃ medinī devī procuate brahmavādibhiḥ. |
tato' bhyupagamād rājñāḥ pṛṭhor vainyasya dhīmataḥ || 108 ||
dubitṛtvam anuprāptā devī pṛṭhvīti cocyate. |
pṛthunā pravibhaktā ca śodhitā ca vasumḍharā || 109 ||
sasyākaravati sphītā purapattanamālinī. || 110 ||
evamprabhāvo vainyaḥ sa rājāsid rājasattamaḥ |
namasyaś caiva pūjyaś ca bhūtagrāmair na samśayaḥ. || 111 ||
brāhmaṇaiś ca mahābhāgair vedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryo brahmayoniḥ sanātanaḥ. || 112 ||
pārthivaiś ca mahābhāgaiḥ pārthivatvam abhīpsubhiḥ |
ādirājo namaskāryaḥ pṛthur vainyaḥ pratāpavān, || 113 ||
yodhair api ca vikrantaiḥ prāptukāmair jayaṃ yudhi. |
ādikartā raṇānāṃ ca | ādikartā raṇānāṃ vai yodhānāṃ prathamo nṛpaḥ. 114 | namasyaḥ pṛthur eva hi. || 114 ||
yo hi yoddhā raṇaṃ yāti kīrtayitvā pṛthuṃ nṛpaṃ, |
sa ghorarūpān samgrāmān kṣemi tarati kīrtimān. || 115 ||
vaiśyair api ca vittādhyair vaiśyavṛttividhāyibhiḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryo vṛttidātā mahāyaśāḥ. || 116 ||
tathaiva śūdraiḥ śucibhiḥ trivarnapariḥāriḥ |
pṛthur eva namaskāryaḥ śreyāḥ param abhipsubhiḥ. || 116 ||
ete vatsaviśeṣās ca dogdhāraḥ kṣiram eva ca |
pātrāṇi ca mayoktāni, kiṃ bhūyo varṇayāmi vaḥ? || 117

## 3. Abschnitt. Manvantara  (Bd. III. 1.3—116; Br. 5.1—64; H. 406—544; Śidh. 58. 2b—82; Vā. 100.3—118;cfr. Mr. 94.)

rṛśaya ūcuḥ: manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi vistareṇa mahāmāte |
teṣāṃ pūrvavisṛṣtiṃ ca lomaharṣaṇa kīrtaya! || 1 ||
yāvanto manavaś caiva yāvantaṃ kālam eva ca |
manvantarāṇi bho sūta śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ! || 2 ||
sūta (lomaharṣaṇa) uvāca: na śakyo vistaro viprā vaktuṃ varṣaśatair api |
manvantarāṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ samkṣepāc chṛnuta dvijāḥ! || 3 ||
svāyaṃbhuvo manuḥ pūrvaṃ manuḥ svārociṣas tathā |
auttamas tāmasaś caiva raivataś cāksuṣas tathā || 4 ||
vaivasvataś ca bho viprāḥ sāṃprataṃ manur ucyate, |
sāvarṇiṣ ca manus tadvad bhautyo raucyas tathaiva ca || 5 ||
tathaiva merusāvarṇāś catvāro manavaḥ smṛtāḥ. |
atītā vartamānāś ca tathavānāgatāś ca ye, || 6 ||
kīritītā manavas tubhyaṃ mayaivaite yathā śrutāḥ. |
ṛṣiṃs teṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi putrān devagaṇāṃs tathā: || 7 ||
maricir atrir bhagavān aṅgirāḥ pulahāḥ kratuḥ |
pulastyas ca vasisthas ca saptaite brahmaṇāḥ sutāḥ. || 8 ||
utarasyāṃ diśi tathā dvijāḥ saptarṣayas tathā. |
yamā nāma tathā devā āsan svāyambhuve 'ntare. || 9 ||
āgnidhras-c-āgnibāhuś ca medhā. medhātithir vasuḥ |
jyotiṣmān dyutimān havyaḥ savanāḥ putra eva ca, || 10 ||
manāḥ svāyambhuvasyaite daśa putrā mahaujasaḥ. |
etad vai prathamaṃ viprā manvantaram udāhṛtaṃ. || 11 ||
aurvo vasisthaputras ca stambhā kāsyapa eva ca |
prāṇo bṛhaspatis caiva | bhārgavas ca tathā prāṇa datto niścyavanas tathā, || 12 || | ṛṣabho 'ṅgirasas tathā |
| paulastyas caiva dattātrir |
| ātreyo niścalas tathā |
| paulaho 'th-ārvarīvāṃś ca, | ete saptarṣayas tathā. || 12 ||
ete maharṣayas tatra vāyuproktā mahāvratāḥ |
devāś ca tuṣitā nāma smṛtāḥ svārociṣe 'ntare. || 13 ||
havidhraḥ sukṛtir, jyotir āpo mūrtir ayasmayaḥ |
prathitaś ca nabhasyas ca nabha ūrjas tathaiva ca || 14 ||
svārociṣasya putrās te manor viprā mahātmanāḥ |
kīrtitāḥ pṛthivipālā mahāviryaparākramāḥ. || 15 ||
dvitīyam etat kathitaṃ viprā manvantaraṃ mayā. |
idaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ! || 16 vasiṣṭhaputrāḥ saptāsan vāsiṣṭhā iti viśrutāḥ |
hiraṇyagarbhasya sutā ūrjā nāma sutejasaḥ. || 17 ||
ṛṣayo 'tra mayā proktāḥ, kīrtyamānān nibodhata |
auttameyān muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrān manoramān: || 18 ||
iṣa ūrjas tanūrjas ca madhur mādhaḥ eṣa ca |
śuciḥ śukraḥ sahaś caiva nabhasyo nabha eṣa ca. || 19 ||
bhānavas tatra devāś ca; manvantaram udāhṛtaṃ. |
manvantaraṃ caturthaṃ tu kathayiṣyāmi sāmprataṃ. || 20 ||
kāvyaḥ pṛṭhus tathaiv-āgnir janyur dhātā dvijottamāḥ |
kapivān akapivāmś ca tatra saptarṣayo dvijāḥ, || 21 ||
purāṇe kīrtitā vipraḥ putrāḥ pautrāś ca bho dvijāḥ. |
satyā devagaṇāś caiva tāmasasyāntare manoḥ. || 22 ||
[putrāmś caiva pravakṣyāmi tāmasasya manor nṛpa: |]
dyutis tapasyaḥ sutapās tapomūlas tapośanāḥ |
taporatir akalmāśas tanvī dhanvī paramtapāḥ. || 23 ||
tāmasasya manor eṣa daśa putrā mahābalāḥ |
vāyuproktā muniśreṣṭhāś, caturthaṃ caitad antaraṃ. || 24 ||
vedabāhur yadudhras ca munir vedaśirās tathā |
hiraṇyaromā parjanya ūrdhvaabāhuś ca somapaḥ || 25 ||
satyanetras tath-atreyā ete saptarṣayo 'pare |
devās c-abbūtarajasas tathā prakṛtayo 'pare || 26 ||
pāriplavas ca raibhyas ca manor antaram ucyate. |
atha putrān imāms tasya budhyadhvaṃ gadato mama: || 27 ||
dhṛtimān avyayo yuktas tattvadarśī nirutsukaḥ |
araṇyas ca prakāśas ca nirmohaḥ satyavān kṛtī, || 28 ||
raivatasya manoḥ putraḥ; pañcamaṃ caitad antaraṃ. |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu saṃpravakṣyāmi, tad budhyadhvaṃ dvijottamāḥ: || 29 ||
bhṛgur nabho vivasvāmś ca sudhāmā virajās tathā |
atināmā sahiṣṇuś ca saptaite tu maharṣayaḥ. || 30 ||
cākṣuṣasyāntare viprā manor devās tv ime smṛtāḥ: |
ādyaḥ prabhūtā ṛbhavaḥ pṛthukās ca divaukasaḥ || 31 ||
lekhās ca nāmato vipraḥ pañca devagaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ. |
ṛṣer aṅgirasaḥ putrā mahātmāno mahaujasaḥ || 32 ||
nādvaleyā muniśreṣṭhā daśa putrās tu viśrutāḥ |
ūruprabhṛtayo viprās cākṣuṣasyāntare manoḥ. || 33 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ manvantaraṃ proktaṃ; saptamaṃ tu nibodhata: |
atrir vasiṣṭho bhagavān kaśyapas ca mahān ṛṣiḥ || 34 ||
gautamo 'tha bharadvājo viśvāmitras tathaiva ca |
tathaiva putro bhagavān rṛcikasya mahātmānaḥ || 35 ||
saptamo jamadagnis ca ṛṣayaḥ sāmprataṃ divi. |
sādhyā rudrās ca viśve ca vasavo marutas tathā || 36 ||
ādityās c-āśvinau caiva devau vaivasvatau smṛtau |
manor vaivasvatasyaite vartante sāmprate 'ntare. || 37 ||
ikṣvākupramukhās caiva (daśa putrā) mahātmanaḥ |
etesāṃ kīrtitānāṃ tu maharṣiṇāṃ mahaujasāṃ. || 38 ||
teṣāṃ putrās ca pautrās ca dikṣu sarvāsu bho dvijāḥ |
manvantaṛeṣu sarveṣu prāg āsan sapta saptakāḥ. || 39 ||
sthitā dharmavyavasthārthaṃ lokasamrakṣaṇāya ca |
manvantare vyatīkrānte catvāraḥ saptakā gaṇāḥ. || 40 ||
kṛtvā karma divaṃ yānti brahmalokam anāmayaṃ. |
tato 'nye tapasā yuktāḥ sthānaṃ tat pūrayanty uta |
atītā vartamānās ca kramenaite bho dvijāḥ. || 41 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ: manvantarāṇi sarvāṇi pūrvāṇy evāparaiḥ saha |
saptarṣiṇām athaiteṣāṃ sāmpratasyāntare manoḥ || 41 ||
vistarāyavavaṃ caiva nisargasya mahātmanaḥ |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca sarvam eva bravihi naḥ. || 41 ||
sūta uvāca: bhavatāṃ kathayisyāmi sarvam etad yathātathaṃ. |
pādaṃ tv imaṃ sasamhāraṃ caturthaṃ munisattamāḥ || 41 ||
manor vaivasvatasyemaṃ sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ |
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca nisargaṃ śṛṇuta dvijāḥ! || 41 ||
manvantarāṇāṃ samkṣepaṃ bhaviṣyaiḥ saha saptabhiḥ |
pralayaṃ caiva lokānāṃ bruvato me nibodhata! || 41 ||
etāny uktāni vai samyak sapta saptasu vai mayā, |
manvantarāṇi saṃkṣepāc chṛṇutānāgatāni me! || 42 ||
sāvarṇā manavo bhavyāḥ pañca, tāṃś ca nibodhata: |
eko vaivasvatas teṣāṃ, catvāras tu prajāpateḥ || 43 ||
parameṣṭhisutā vipra merusāvarṇatāṃ gatāḥ, |
dakṣasyaite hi dauhitrāḥ priyāyās tanayā nṛpāḥ, || 44 ||
mahatā tapasā yukta merupṛṣṭhe mahaujasaḥ. |
ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāma manuḥ smṛtaḥ, |
bhūtyāṃ cotpādito devyāṃ bhautyo nāma ruceḥ sutaḥ. || 45 ||
sāvarṇasya pravakṣyāmi manor vaivasvatasya ha |
bhaviṣyasya bhaviṣyaṃ tu samāsāt, tan nibodhata! || 45 ||
anāgatās ca saptaite smṛtā divi maharṣayaḥ |
manor antaram āsādyā sāvarṇasyeha bho dvijāḥ: || 46 ||
rāmo vyāsas tath-ātreyo diptimān iti viśrutaḥ |
kauśiko galavaś caiva jāmadagnyaś ca bhārgavaḥ || 46 ||
dvaipāyano vasisthaś ca kṛpaḥ śāradvatas tathā |
ātreyo diptimāṃś caiva ṛṣyaśṛṅgas tu kāśyapaḥ |
bhāradvājas tathā drauṇir aśvatthāmā mahādyutiḥ || 47 ||
gautamasyatmajaś caiva śaradvān nāma gautamaḥ |
kauśiko gālavaś caiva ruruḥ kāśyapa eva ca, |
ete sapta mahātmāno bhaviṣyā munisattamāḥ. || 48 ||
brahmaṇaḥ sadṛśās caite dhanyāḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
abbijātyātha tapasā mantravyākaraṇais tathā. || 481 ||
brahmalokapratiṣṭhās tu smṛtā brahmarṣayo 'malāḥ |
bhūtabhavyabhava[j]jñānaṃ buddhvā caiva ca ye svayaṃ, || 482 ||
tapasā tu prasiddhā ye saṃgatāḥ pravicintakāḥ |
mantravyākaraṇādyais ca aiśvaryāt sarvaśas ca ye, || 483 ||
etān bhāryānvito jñātvā naiṣṭhikāni ca nāma ca |
saptaite saptabhiś caiva guṇaiḥ saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ, || 484 ||
dirghāyuṣo mantrakṛta iśvarā dirghacakṣuṣaḥ |
buddhyā pratyakṣadharmāno gotraprāvartakās tathā. || 485 ||
kṛtādiṣu yugākhyeṣu sarveṣv eva punaḥ punaḥ |
sutapās c-āmitābhās ca mukhyās caiva gaṇās trayāḥ, |
teṣāṃ gaṇās tu devānāṃ ekaiko viṃśakaḥ smṛtāḥ. || 481 ||
nāmatas tu pravakṣyāmi nibodhadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ: |
ṛtus tapas ca śukraś ca dyutir jyotiḥ prabhākaraḥ || 482 ||
prabhāso bhāsakṛd dharmaś tejo raśmiḥ kratur virāṭ |
arciṣmān dyotano bhānur yaśaḥ kirtir budho dhṛtiḥ, |
viṃśatiḥ sutapā hy ete nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 483 ||
prabhur vibhur vibhāsaś ca jetā hant-ārihā ṛtuḥ |
sumatiḥ pramatiṛ dīptiḥ samākhyaṭo maho mahān || 484 ||
deho munir nayo jyeṣṭhaḥ samāḥ satyaś ca viśrutāḥ, |
ity ete hy amitābhās tu viṃśatiḥ parikīrtitāḥ. || 485 ||
pravartayanti te varṇān āśramāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ || 486 ||
saptarṣayo mahābhāgāḥ satyadharmaparāyaṇāḥ. |
teṣāṃ caivānvayotpannā jāyantiḥa punaḥ punaḥ || 487 ||
mantrabrāhmaṇakartāro dharme praśithile tathā. |
yasmāc ca varadāḥ sapra parebhya eva yācitāḥ, || 488 ||
tasmān na kālo na vayāḥ pramāṇaṃ ṛṣibhāvane. |
ete(!) saptarṣikoddeśo vyākhyātas te mayā nṛpa. || 489 ||
māricasyaiva te putraḥ kaśyapasya mahātmanaḥ || 49 ||
| sāmpratasya bhaviṣyanti sāvarṇasyāntare manoḥ, |
| teṣām indro bhaviṣyas tu balir vairocanaḥ purā. || 491 ||
viravāṃś c-āvariyāṃś ca sumanto dhṛtimān vasuḥ |
cariṣṇur āryo dhṛṣṇuś ca vājā sumatir eva ca |
sāvarṇasya manoḥ putrā bhaviṣyā munisattamā. || 50 ||
| nava cānyeṣu vakṣyāmi sāvarṇeṣv antaṛeṣu vai. |
| sāvarṇamanavās cānye bhaviṣyā brahmanaḥ sutāḥ || 501 ||
merusāvarṇitas te vai catvāro divyadṛṣṭayaḥ. |
dakṣasya te hi dauhitraḥ kriyāyā duhituḥ sutāḥ, || 502 ||
mahatā tapasā yuktā merupṛṣṭhe mahaujasaḥ |
brahmādibhis te janitā dakṣenaiva ca dhimatā. || 503 ||
maharlokaṃ gatāvṛttā bhaviṣyā merum āśritāḥ |
mahānubhāvās te pūrvaṃ jajñire cākṣuṣe 'ntare: || 504 ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ: dakṣeṇa janitāḥ putraḥ kanyāyām ātmanaḥ kathaṃ |
bhavena brahmanā caiva dharmeṇa ca mahātmanaḥ(!)? || 505 ||
sūta uvāca: ato bhaviṣyān vakṣyāmi sāvarṇamanavas tu ye, |
teṣāṃ janma prabhāvaṃ ca namas kṛtvā pracetase. || 506 ||
vaivasvate hy upaspṛṣṭe kimcicchiṣṭe ca cākṣuṣe |
jajñire manavas te hi bhaviṣyānāgatāntare || 507 ||
prācetāsasya dakṣasya dauhitrā manavas tu ye |
sāvarṇā nāmataḥ pañca catvāraḥ paramarṣijāḥ. || 508 ||
samjñā-putras tu sāvarṇa eko vaivasvatas tathā |
jyeṣṭhaḥ samjñā-suto nāma manur vaivasvataḥ prabhuḥ, || 509 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare prāpte samutpattis tayoḥ śubhā. |
caturdaśaite manavaḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ || 5010 ||
vede smṛtau purāṇe ca sarve te prabhaviṣṇavaḥ |
prajānāṃ patayaḥ sarve bhūtānāṃ patayaḥ sthitāḥ. || 5011 ||
tair iyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā saptadvipā sapattanā |
pūṇaṃ yugasahasraṃ vai paripālyā nareśvaraiḥ || 5012 ||
prajābhis tapasā caiva. vistaraṃ teṣu vakṣyate. |
caturdaśaiva te jñeyāḥ sargāḥ svāyambhuvādayaḥ. || 5013 ||
manvantarādikāreṣu vartante 'tra sakṛt sakṛt |
vinivṛttādikārās te maharlokaṃ samāśritāḥ || 5014 ||
samatitās tu ye teṣām aṣṭau ṣaṭ ca tathāpare |
pūrvेषu, sāṃprataś cāyaṃ śāṣṭi vaivasvataḥ prabhuḥ. || 5015 ||
ye śiṣṭās, tān pravakṣyāmi saha devarṣi-dānavaiḥ |
saha prajānisargeṇa sarvāms tv anāgatān dvijān). || 5016 ||
vaivasvatanisargeṇa teṣāṃ jñeyas tu vistaraḥ, |
anūnā) nātiriktās te: yasmāt sarve vivasvataḥ. || 5017 ||
punaruktabahutvāt tu na vakṣye teṣu vistaraṃ |
manvantareṣu bhavyeṣu bhūtesv api tathaiva ca. || 5018 ||
kule kule nisargā) tu tasmāj jñeyā) vibhāgaśaḥ, |
teṣām eva hi siddhyarthaṃ vistareṇa krameṇa ca. || 5019 ||
dakṣasya kanyā dharmiṣṭhā suvratā nāma viśrutā |
sarvakanyāvarīṣṭhā) tu jyeṣṭhā yā viriñīsutā, || 5020 ||
gṛhītvā tāṃ pitā kanyāṃ jagāma brahmaṇo 'ntike |
vairājastham) upāsinaṃ dharmeṇa ca bhavena tu. || 5021 ||
bhava-dharma-samipasthaṃ dakṣaṃ brahmābhyaḥśata: |
dakṣa kanyā taveyaṃ vai janayiṣyati suvratā) || 5022 ||
caturo vai manūn putrāṃś cāturvarṇyakarāñ chubhān. |
brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣo dharmo bhavas tadā || 5023 ||
tāṃ kanyāṃ manasā jagmus trayas te brahmaṇā saha. |
satyābhidhyāyināṃ teṣāṃ sadyaḥ kanyā vyajāyata || 5024 ||
sadṛśān) rūpatas) teṣāṃ caturo vai kumārakān. |
saṃsiddhāḥ kāryakaraṇe saṃbhūtās te śriyānvitāḥ || 5025 ||
upabhogāsamarthais ca sadyojātaḥ śarīrakaiḥ. |
te dṛṣtvā tān svayaṃ buddhvā) brahmavyāhāriṇas tadā || 5026 ||
saṃrabdhā vai vyakaraṣanta mama putro mamety uta |
abhidhyāyātmanotpannān ūcur vai te paraspam: || 5027 ||
yo yasya vapuṣā tulyo, bhajatāṃ sa tu taṃ sutaṃ; |
yasya yaḥ sadṛśas cāpi rūpe vīrye ca nāmataḥ, | 5028 ||
taṃ gṛhñātu sa), bhadraṃ vo, varṇato yasya yaḥ samaḥ. |
dhruvaṃ rūpaṃ pituḥ putraḥ so 'nurudhyati sarvadā, || 5029 ||
tasmād ātmasamaḥ putraḥ pitur mātuṣ ca vīryataḥ. |
evaṃ te samayaṃ krītvā savarnā) jagṛhuḥ sutān. || 5030 ||
yasmāt savarnāṣ teṣāṃ vai brahmādināṃ kumārakāḥ, |
savarnā manavas tasmāt savarnatvaṃ ca teṣu tat. || 5031 ||
mananān mānasāc caiva tasmāt te manavaḥ smṛtāḥ. |
cākṣuṣasyāntare 'tite prāpte vaivasvatasya ha || 5032 ||
ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāmābhavat sutaḥ |
bhūtyām utpādito yas tu bhautyo nāma kaveḥ sutaḥ. || 5033 ||
vaivasvate 'ntare jātau dvau manū tu vivasvataḥ: |
vaivasvato manur yaś ca sāvarṇo yaś ca viśrutaḥ. || 5034 ||
jyeṣṭhaḥ samjñāsuto vidvān manur vaivasvataḥ prabhuḥ, |
savarnāyāḥ sutas cānyaḥ smṛto vaivasvato manuḥ. || 5035 ||
sāvarṇa-manavo ye ca catvāras tu maharṣijāḥ |
tapasā saṃbhṛtātmānaḥ sveṣu manvantareṣu vai || 5036 ||
bhaviṣyeṣu bhaviṣyanti sarvakāryārthasādhakāḥ. |
prathamaṃ merusāvarṇiṃ pravakṣyāmi, munīn śṛṇu |
pārā maricigarbhās ca sudharmāṇas ca te trayaḥ |
saṃbhūtās ca mahātmānaḥ sarve vaivasvate 'ntare. || 5038 ||
dakṣaputrasya putrās te rohitasya prajāpateḥ |
bhaviṣyanti bhaviṣyās tu, ekaiko dvādaśo gaṇaḥ: || 5039 ||
aiśvaras ca graho rāhur vākur vaṃṣas tathaiva ca |
pārā dvādaśa vijñeyā, uttarāṃs tu nibodhata: || 5040 ||
vājipo vājijic caiva prabhūtīs ca kakud yathā |
dadhikrāvā vipakkvas ca praṇito vijayo madhuḥ || 5041 ||
utathy-ottamakau dvau tu dvādaśaite maricayaḥ. |
sudharmanas tu vakṣyāmi, nāmatas tān nibodhata: || 5042 ||
varnas tath-athagarviś ca bhuraṇyo vrajano mataḥ) |
amito dravaketuś ca jambho 'th-ajas tu) śakrakaḥ) || 504s ||
sunemir dyutayaś) caiva sudharmāṇaḥ prakīrtitāḥ. |
teṣām indras tadā bhavyo hy adbhuto nāma nāmataḥ. || 5044 ||
skando 'sau pārvaṭiyo) vai kārtikeyas tu pāvakiḥ) |
medhātithiś ca) paulastyo vasuḥ kāśyapa eva ca |
jyotiṣmān bhārgavaś) caiva dyutimān aṅgirās tathā || 51 ||
savanaś) caiva vāsiṣṭha ātreyo havyavāhanaḥ) |
paulahaḥ satya) ity ete munayo) rohite 'ntare) || 52 ||
devatānāṃ gaṇās tatra traya eva narādhipa |
dakṣaputrasya putrās te rohitasya prajāpateḥ. || 521 ||
dhṛṣṭaketur) diptiketuḥ) pañcahasto) nirāmayaḥ) |
pṛthuśravā) bhūridyumno) ṛciḥko vṛhato) gayaḥ || 53 ||
prathamasya tu sāvarṇer nava putrā mahaujasaḥ). |
daśame tv atha paryāye dviṭiyasyāntare manoḥ || 54 ||
daśame tv atha paryāye dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ |
dviṭiyasya tu sāvarṇer bhāvyasyaivāntare manoḥ: || 54 ||
| sudhāmāno viruddhās ca dvāv eva tu gaṇau smṛtau |
tviṣimantaś ca te sarve śatasamkhyās ca te samāḥ, || 54 ||
prāṇānāṃ yac chataṃ proktaṃ ṛṣibhiḥ puruṣeṣu vai, |
devās te vai bhaviṣyanti dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ, |
teṣām indras tathā vidvān bhaviṣyaḥ śāntir ucyate. || 54. ||
haviṣmān paulahaś caiva sukṛtiś caiva bhārgavaḥ |
āpomūrtis tath-ātreyo vasiṣṭhaś c-āpavaḥ smṛtaḥ || 55 ||
paulastyo 'pratimaś caiva nābhāgaś caiva kāśyapaḥ |
āngirā nabhasaḥ satyaḥ saptaite paramaṣayaḥ. || 56 ||
| devatānāṃ gaṇau dvau tāv ṛṣi-mantrās ca ye smṛtāḥ |
sukṣetraś c-ottamaujās ca bhūriṣeṇās ca vīryavān |
śatāniko nirāmitro vṛṣaseno jayadrathaḥ || 57 ||
bhūridyumnaḥ suvarcās ca daśa tv ete manoḥ sutāḥ. |
ekādaśe tu paryāye ekādaśe tu paryāye tṛṭiyasyāntare manoḥ || 58 ||
sāvarṇe vai tṛṭiyake || 58 ||
| nirmāṇaratayo devāḥ kāmajā vai manojavāḥ |
gaṇās tv ete trayāḥ khyatā devatānāṃ mahātmanāṃ. || 58 ||
| ekaikas trimśatas teṣāṃ gaṇas tu tridivaukasāṃ. |
māsasyāhāni trimśat tu yāni vai kavayo viduḥ, || 582 ||
nirmāṇa'ratayo devā, rātrayas tu vihaṃgamāḥ. |
gaṇas tṛtīyo yaḥ prokto devatānāṃ bhaviṣyati, || 583 ||
manojavā muhūrtās tu iti devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. |
ete hi brahmaṇaḥ putrā bhaviṣyā mānavāḥ smṛtāḥ || 584 ||
teṣām indro vṛṣo nāma bhaviṣyaḥ surarāt tataḥ. || 585 ||
tasya sapta ṛṣiṃś cāpi kīrtyamānān nibodhata: |
haviṣmān kāṣyapaś cāpi vapuṣmāmś caiva bhārgavaḥ || 59 ||
vāruniś ca tath-ātreyo vāsiṣṭho 'nagha eva ca |
puṣṭiś c-āngiraso jñeyaḥ paulastyo niścaras tathā || 60 ||
paulahaś c-āgnitejās ca | paulaho hy agnitejās ca bhāvyāḥ sapta maharṣayaḥ. | devā hy ekādaśe 'ntare. || 61 ||
brahmaṇas tu sutā devā, gaṇas teṣāṃ trayaḥ smṛtāḥ. || 61 ||
sarvātragaḥ suśarmā ca devānīkaḥ purovahaḥ |
kṣemadhanvā dṛdhāyuś ca ādarśaḥ paunḍrako manuḥ || 62 ||
sāvarṇasya tu putrā vai tṛṭiyasya nava smṛṭāḥ. |
caturthasya tu sāvarṇer ṛṣin sapta nibodha me: || 63 ||
sāvarṇasya tu te putrāḥ prājāpatyasya vai nava. |
dvādaśe tv atha paryāye rudraputrasya vai manoḥ || 63 ||
caturtho rudrasāvarṇo, devāṃs tasyāntare śṛṇu: |
pañcaiva tu gaṇāḥ proktā devatānām anāgatāḥ: || 631 ||
haritā rohitās caiva devāḥ sumanasas tathā |
sukarmāṇāḥ sutārās ca pañca devagaṇāḥ smṛṭāḥ. || 632 ||
brahmaṇo mānasā hy eta ekaiko daśako gaṇāḥ: |
aruntijo haviṣ caiva vidvān yaś ca sahasradāḥ || 633 ||
parvato 'nucaras caiva apāṃsūs ca manojavāḥ |
ūrjā svāhā svadhā tārā, daśaite haritāḥ smṛṭāḥ. || 634 ||
tapo jñānī mṛtiṣ caiva varcā bandhuṣ ca yaḥ smṛṭāḥ |
rajaś caiva tu rājaś ca svarṇapādas tathaiva ca || 635 ||
vyuṣṭir vidhiṣ ca vai devā daśaite rohitāḥ smṛṭāḥ. |
tuṣitādyās tu ye devās trayastrimśat prakirititāḥ, || 636 ||
devān sumanaso vidyān. nibodhata sukarmaṇāḥ: |
suparvā vṛṣabhaḥ pṛṣṭā kapi-dyumna. vipaścitāḥ || 637 ||
vikramaś ca kramaś caiva vibhṛtaḥ kānta eva ca, |
ete devāḥ sukarmāṇāḥ. sutārāmś ca nibodhata: || 638 ||
varṣo divyas tath-āñjiṣṭho varcasvī dyutimān kaviḥ |
śubho haviḥ kṛtaprāptir vyāpṛṭo daśamas tathā, || 639 ||
sutārā nāmatas tv ete devā vai samprakirititāḥ. |
teṣām indras tu vijñeya ṛṭadhāmā mahāyaśāḥ. || 6310 ||
dyutir vaṣiṣṭhaputras tu ātreyaḥ sutapās tathā |
tapomūrtis tv āngirasas tapasvī kāśyapas tathā || 64 ||
tapodhanaś ca paulastyaḥ paulahas ca taporatiḥ |
bhārgavaḥ saptamas teṣāṃ vijñeyas tu tapodhṛtiḥ. || 65 ||
pañca devaganāḥ proktā | ete saptarṣayaḥ siddhā mānasā brahmanāḥ sutāḥ. | antye sāvarṇike 'ntare. |
devavān upadevaś ca devaśreṣṭho vidūrathaḥ || 66 ||
mitravān mitravinduś ca mitraseno hy amitrahā mitrabāhuḥ suvarcāś ca dvādaśasya manoḥ sutāḥ. || 67 ||
trayodaše tu paryāye bhāvye raucyantare punaḥ || 68 ||
traya eva gaṇāḥ proktā devānāṃ tu svayambhuvā, |
brahmano mānasāḥ putrās te hi sarve mahātmanaḥ: || 68 ||
sutramāṇaḥ sudharmāṇaḥ sukarmāṇas ca te trayaḥ |
tridaśanāṃ gaṇāḥ proktā bhaviṣyāḥ somapāyināṃ. || 68 ||
trayastrimśad devatā yāḥ pṛthag iṣṭas tu yājñikaiḥ |
ājyena pṛṣadājyena grahaśreṣṭhena caiva hi. || 68 ||
ye vai devās trayastrimśat, pṛthaktvena nibodhata: |
sutramāṇaḥ prayājyās tu, ājyapā ye tu sāṃprataṃ, || 68 ||
sukarmāṇo 'nuyājyās tu, pṛṣadājyāśinas tu ye |
upayājyāḥ sudharmāṇa iti devāḥ prakīrtitāḥ. || 68 ||
divaspatir mahāsattvas teṣām indro bhaviṣyati. |
pulahātmajaputrās te vijñeyās tu ruceḥ sutāḥ: || 68 ||
aṅgirās caiva dhṛtimān paulastyo 'py avyayas tu yaḥ |
paulahas tattvadarśī ca bhārgavaś ca nirutsukaḥ || 69 ||
nisprakampas tath-atreyo nirmohaḥ kāsyapas tathā |
sutapās caiva vāsisthaḥ saptaite tu maharṣayaḥ. || 70 ||
| traya eva gaṇāḥ proktā devatānāṃ svayambhuvā. |
| trayodaśasya putrās te vijñeyās tu ruceḥ sutāḥ: || 70 ||
citraseno vicitrās ca nayo dharmabhṛto dhṛtaḥ |
sunetraḥ kṣatravṛddhiś ca sutapā nirbhayo dṛḍhaḥ, || 71 ||
raucyasyaite manoḥ putrā antare tu trayodaśe. |
caturdaśe tu paryāye bhautasyaivāntare manoḥ || 72 ||
| devatānāṃ gaṇāḥ pañca proktā ye tu bhaviṣyanti |
cākṣuṣās ca pavitrās ca kanisthā bhrajirās tathā || 72 ||
vācāvṛddhās ca, ity ete pañca devagaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ. |
niṣādādyāḥ svarāḥ sapta sapta tān vidhī cākṣuṣān, || 72 ||
bṛhadādyāni samāni kanisthān sapta tān viduḥ, |
sapta lokāḥ pavitrās te, bhrajirāḥ sapta sindhavaḥ, || 72 ||
vācāvṛddhān ṛṣin vidhī manoḥ svāyambhuvasya vai. |
sarve manvantarendrās ca vijñeyās tulyalakṣaṇāḥ || 72 ||
tejasā tapasā buddhyā balaśrutaparākrāmaḥ. |
trailokye yāni sattvāni gatimanti dhruvāni ca, || 72 ||
sarvaśāḥ svair guṇais, tāni indrās te 'bhibhavanti vai |
bhūtāpavādino hṛṣṭā madhyasthā bhūtavādinaḥ |
bhūtānuvādinaḥ saktās trayo vedāḥ pravādināṃ. || 72 ||
agnidhraḥ kāśyapaś caiva paulastyo māgadhaś ca yaḥ |
bhārgavo hy agnibāhuś ca śucir āngirasas tathā || 73 ||
yuktaś caiva tath-ātreyaḥ śukro vāsistha eva ca |
ajitaḥ paulahaś caiva antyāḥ saptarṣayaś ca ye. || 74 ||
eteṣāṃ kalyam utthāya kīrtanāt sukhā edhate |
yaśaś cāpnoti sumahad āyuṣmāṃś ca bhavet tathā. || 75 ||
atītānāgatānāṃ vai maharṣiṇāṃ sadā narāḥ |
devatānāṃ gaṇāḥ proktāḥ pañca vai bharatarṣabha || 76 ||
tarāṅgabhīrur bradhnaś ca tarasm-ānugro eva ca |
abhimāni pravīnaś ca jiṣnuḥ saṃkrandanas tathā || 77 ||
tejasvi sabalaś caiva bhautyasyaite manoḥ sutāḥ. |
sāvarṇā manavo hy ete catvāro brahmanāḥ sutāḥ || 78 ||
eko vaivasvataś caiva sāvarṇo manur ucyate |
raucyo bhautyas ca yau tau tu matau paulaha-bhārgavau. |
bhautyasyaivādhiḥkare tu pūrṇe kalpas tu pūryate. || 78 ||
ity ete 'nāgatātītā manavaḥ kīrtitā mayā; |
tair iyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā samudranta sapattanā || 79 ||
pūrṇaṃ yugasahasraṃ tu paripālyā dvijottamāḥ prajābhis tapasā caiva, saṃhāras teṣu nityaśaḥ. || 80 ||
yugāni saptatis tāni sāgrāni kathitāni ca |
kṛta-tretādiyuktāni manor antaram ucyate || 81 ||
caturdaśaite manavaḥ kīrtitāḥ kīrtivardhanāḥ |
vedeṣu sapurāneṣu sarveṣu prabhaviṣṇavaḥ || 82 ||
prajānāṃ patayo viprā dhanyam eṣāṃ ca kīrtanaṃ. |
manvantaṛeṣu saṃhārāḥ saṃhārānteṣu saṃbhavāḥ || 83 ||
na śakyam antaṃ teṣāṃ vai vaktuṃ varṣaśatair api. |
visargasya prajānāṃ vai saṃhārasya ca bho dvijāḥ || 84 ||
manvantaṛeṣu saṃhārāḥ śrūyante dvijasattamāḥ. |
saśeṣās tatra tiṣṭhanti devāḥ saptārṣibhiḥ saha || 85 ||
tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śrutena ca samanvitāḥ. |
pūrṇe yugasahasre tu kalpo niḥṣeṣa ucyate, || 86 ||
tatra sarvāṇi bhūtāni dagdhāny ādityaraśmibhiḥ. |
brahmāṇam agrataḥ kṛtvā sahādityagaṇair dvijāḥ || 87 ||
praviṣanti suraśreṣṭhaṃ hariṃ nārāyaṇaṃ prabhuṃ |
sraṣṭāraṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ kalpānteṣu punaḥ punaḥ || 88 ||
avyaktāḥ śāśvato devas, tasya sarvam idaṃ jagat. |
atra vaḥ kīrtayiṣyāmi manor vaivasvatasya vai || 89 ||
niṣargaṃ munīṣardūlāḥ saṃpratasya mahādyuteḥ |
atra vaṃśaprasaṅgena kathyamānaṃ purātanaṃ, || 90 ||
yatrotpanno mahātmā sa harir vṛṣṇikule prabhuḥ |
sarvāsuravināśāya sarvalokahitāya ca. || 91 ||

## 4. Abschnitt. Vaṃśānucarita | 1. Kapitel

vivasvān kaśyapāj jajñe dāksāyaṇyāṃ mahāyaśāḥ, |
tasya bhāryābhavat samjñā tvāṣṭrī devī vivasvataḥ, || 1 ||
sureśvarīti vikhyātā | sureṇur iti vikhyātā triṣu lokeṣu bhāvinī. | punaḥ samjñeti viśrutā. |
sā vai bhāryā bhagavato mārtaṇḍasya mahātmanaḥ. || 2 ||
bharṭṛrūpeṇa nātuṣyad rūpayauvanaśālīnī, |
| [samjñā nāma sutapa-ā sudīptena samanvitā] ādityasya hi tad rūpaṃ maṇḍalasya sutejasā || 3 ||
gātreṣu paridaḍdhaṃ vai nātīkāntam ivābhavat. |
na khalv ayaṃ mṛto 'ṇḍastha, iti snebād abhaṣata || 4 ||
ajānan kaśyapas, tasmān mārtaṇḍa iti cocyate. |
tejas tv abhyadhikaṃ tasya nityam eva vivasvataḥ, || 5 ||
yenātitāpayāmāsa trīṃl lokān kaśyapātmajaḥ. |
trīṃy apatyāni bho vipraḥ | trīṃy apatyāni samjñāyāṃ samjñāyāṃ tapatāṃ varaḥ || 6 || | janayāmāsa vai raviḥ || 6 ||
ādityo janayāmāsa | dvau sutau tu mahāvīryau kanyāṃ dvau ca prajāpati: | kanyāṃ kalindim eva ca |
manur vaivasvataḥ pūrvaṃ śrāddhadevaḥ prajāpatiḥ || 7 ||
yamaś ca yamunā caiva | tato yamo yamī caiva yamajau sambabhūvatuḥ. | yamajau sambabhūvatuḥ. |
śyāma varṇaṃ tu tad rūpaṃ dṛṣtvā samjñā vivasvataḥ || 8 ||
asahantī tu svāṃ chāyāṃ savarṇāṃ nirmame tataḥ |
māyāmayī tu sā samjñā, | mahīmayī tu sā nārī, tasyāṃ chāyā samutthita. || 9 || | tasyās chāyā samudgatā. || 9 ||
prāñjaliḥ praṇatā bhūtvā chāyā samjnāṃ dvijottamaḥ |
nvāca: kiṃ mayā kāryaṃ, kathayasva śucismite! |
sthitāsmi tava nirdeśe, śādhi māṃ varavarṇini! || 10 ||
samjñovāca: ahaṃ yāsyāmi, bhadraṃ te, svam eva bhavanaṃ pituḥ, |
tvayaiva bhavane mahyaṃ vastavyaṃ nirvikārayā. || 11 ||
imau ca bālakau mahyaṃ kanyā ceyaṃ sumadhyamā |
saṃbhavyās te na cākhyeyam idaṃ bhagavate tvayā. || 12 ||
savarṇovāca: ā keśagrahaṇād devi ā śapān naiva karhicit |
ākhyāsyāmi mataṃ tubbyaṃ, gaccha devi yathāsukhaṃ! || 13 ||
saṃādiśya savarṇaṃ tu tathety uktā tayā ca sā |
tvaṣṭuḥ samiṣam agamad vriḍiteva tapasvini. || 14 ||
pituḥ samiṣagā sā tu pitrā nirbhartsitā tadā: |
bhartuḥ samipaṃ gaccheti niyuktā ca punaḥ punaḥ. || 15 ||
agacchad vaḍavā bhūtvācchādyā rūpam aninditā; |
kurūn athottarān gatvā tṛṇāny atha cacāra ha. || 16 ||
dviṭiyāyāṃ tu samjñāyāṃ samjñeyam iti cintayan |
ādityo janayāmāsa putram ātmasamaṃ tadā. || 17 ||
pūrvajasya manor vipraḥ sadṛṣo 'yam iti prabhuḥ, |
manur evābhavan nāmnā sāvarṇir iti cocyate. || 18 ||
dvitīyo yaḥ sutas tasyāḥ, sa vijñeyaḥ śanaiścaraḥ. |
samjñā tu pārthivī sā vai) svasa putrasya vai tadā || 19 ||
cakārābhyadhikaṃ snehaṃ na tathā pūrvajeṣu vai). |
manus tasyāḥkṣamat, tat tu) yamas tasyā na cakṣame, || 20 ||
sa vai roṣāc) ca bālyāc ca bhāvino 'ṛthasya vai balāt) |
padā) saṃtarjayāmāsa samjñām) vaivasvato yamaḥ). || 21 ||
taṃ) śaśāpa tataḥ krodhāt sāvarṇajanani tadā): |
carañaḥ pataṭām eṣa taveti) bhṛṣaduḥkhitā). || 22 ||
śrutaśravā manus tābhyām) |
sāvarṇir vai) bhaviṣyati || 18 ||
śrutakarmā tu vijñeyo, graho vai yaḥ) śanaiścaraḥ. |
samjñā tu pārthivī teṣāṃ ātmajānāṃ yathākarot, || 19 ||
na sneham) pūrvajātānām) tathā kṛtavatī tu sā). |
padā) tarjayase) yasmāt) pitṛbhāryāṃ yaśasvinīm), |
tasmāt tavaiṣa carañaḥ patiṣyati na saṃśayaḥ. || 22 ||
yamas tu tat pituḥ sarvaṃ prāñjaliḥ pratyavedayat |
yamas tu tena śāpena bhṛśaṃ pīḍitamānasaḥ |
manunā saha dharmātmā pituḥ sarvaṃ nyavedayat |
bhṛśaṃ śāpabhayodvignaḥ samjñāvākyair vivejitaḥ: || 23 ||
śāpo 'yaṃ vinivarteta, sa prāha pitaraṃ tada, |
mātrā snehena sarveṣu vartitavyaṃ suteṣu vai. || 24 ||
snehena tulyam asmāsu mātā deva na vartate. || 24 ||
seyam asmān apahāya kanīyāṃsaṃ bubhūṣati. |
tasyāṃ mayodyataḥ pādo, na tu dehe nipātitaḥ. || 25 ||
bālyād vā yadi vā mohāt tad bhavān kṣantum arhati! |
śapto 'ham asmi lokeśa jananyā tapatāṃ vara, |
tava prasādāc carāno na paten mama gopate! || 26 ||
vivasvān uvāca: asaṃṣayaṃ putra mahad bhaviṣyaty atra kāraṇaṃ, |
yena tvām āviṣat krodho dharmajñaṃ satyavādinaṃ. || 27 ||
na śakya etan mithyā tu kartuṃ mātur vacas tava, |
kṛmayo māṃsam ādāya yāsyanti tu mahītale. || 28 ||
tataḥ pādāṃ mahāprāṇīna punaḥ saṃprāpsyase sukhāṃ; |
kṛtam evaṃ vacaḥ satyaṃ māṃsus tava bhaviṣyati, || 29 ||
śāpasya parihāreṇa tvaṃ ca trāto bhaviṣyasi. |
ādityas tv abravīt samjñāṃ: kimarthaṃ tanayeṣu vai || 30 ||
tulyeṣv abhyadhikāḥ sneha ekasmin kriyate tvayā? |
sā tat pariharanti tu nācacakṣe vivasvataḥ. || 31 ||
ātmanāṃ sa samādhāya yogāt tathyaṃ apaśyata, |
tāṃ śaptukāmo bhagavān nāsāya kupitaḥ prabhuḥ || 32 ||
| mūrdhajeṣu ca jagrāha; samaye 'tigate tu sā tataḥ sarvaṃ yathāvṛttam ācacakṣe vivasvataḥ. ||
vivasvān atha tac chrutvā kruddhas tvaṣṭāram abhyagāt. || 33 ||
tvaṣṭā tu taṃ yathānyāyam arcayitvā vibhāvasuṃ |
nirdagdhukāmaṃ roṣeṇa sāntvayāmāsa vai tada. || 34 ||
tvaṣṭovāca: tavātitejasāviṣṭam idaṃ rūpaṃ na śobbhate, |
asahanti tu tat samjñā vane carati śāḍvale. || 35 ||
drakṣyate tāṃ bhavān adya svāṃ bhāryāṃ śubhacāriṇīṃ |
ślāghyaṃ yogabalopetāṃ |
yogam āsthāya gopate. || 36 ||
anuktūlaṃ tu te deva |
yadi syān, mama yan mataṃ, |
rūpaṃ nivartayāmy adya tava kāntam ariṃdama. || 37 ||
tato 'bhyupagamāt tvaṣṭā mārtanḍasya vivasvataḥ bhrānim āropya tat tejaḥ śātayāmāsa tasya vai. || 38 ||
tato nirbhāsitaṃ rūpaṃ tejasā samhatena tu |
kāntāt kāntataraṃ draṣṭum adhikaṃ śuśubhe tadā. || 39 ||
dadarśa yogam āsthāya svāṃ bhāryāṃ vaḍavāṃ tathā |
adhṛṣyaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ tejasā niyamena ca || 40 ||
| vaḍavāvapuṣā viprāś carantīm akutobhayāṃ. |
so 'śvarūpeṇa mārtanḍas tāṃ mukhe samabhāvyat, |
maithunāya viceṣṭantīṃ parapuṃsopaśāṅkayā. || 41 ||
sā tan niravamac chukraṃ nāsikābhyāṃ vivasvataḥ. |
devau tasyām ajāyetām aśvinau bhiṣajāṃ varau: || 42 ||
nāsatyaś caiva dasraś ca smṛtau dvāṃ aśvināṃ iti |
mārtanḍasya sutāṃ etāṃ aṣṭamasya prajāpateḥ. || 43 ||
tāṃ tu rūpeṇa kāntena darśayāmāsa bhāskaraḥ, |
sā tu dṛṣṭvaiva bhartāraṃ tutoṣa ca mumoha ca. || 44 ||
yamas tu tena śāpena bhṛṣaṃ pīḍitamānasaḥ |
dharmeṇa rañjayāmāsa dharmarāja imāḥ prajāḥ. || 45 ||
sa lebhe karmaṇā tena śubhena paramadyutiḥ |
pitṛṇām ādhipatyaṃ ca lokapālatvam eva ca. || 46 ||
manuḥ prajāpatis tv asīt sāvarṇiḥ sa mahayāśaḥ |
bhāvyaḥ so 'nāgate tasmin manuḥ sāvarṇike 'ntare. || 47 ||
rayat; śidh. mukhato nāsikāyāṃ tu śukraṃ tan nyadadhan mune. merupṛṣṭhe tapo ghoram adyapi carate prabhuḥ. |
bhrātā śanaiścaras tasya grahatvaṃ sa tu labdhavān. || 48 ||
tvaṣṭā tu tejasā tena viṣṇoś cakram akalpayat, |
tad apratihataṃ yuddhe dānavāntacikīrṣayā. || 49 ||
yaviyasī tayor yā tu yamī kanyā yaśasvinī, |
abhavac sā saricchreṣṭhā yamunā lokapāvanī. || 50 ||
manur ity ucyate loke | yas tu jyeṣṭho mahātejā sāvarṇa iti cocyate, | sargo yasya tu sāmprataṃ, |
dvitiyo yaḥ sutas tasya | vistaraṃ tasya vaksyāmi manor bhrātā śanaiścaraḥ, | manor vaivasvatasya ha. || 52 ||
grahatvaṃ sa tu lebhe vai sarvalokābhipūjitaḥ. || 50 ||
ya idaṃ janma devānāṃ śṛṇuyād vā paṭheta vā |
āpadaṃ prāpya mucyeta prāpnuyāc ca mahad yaśaḥ. || 51 ||

## 4. Abschnitt. Vaṃśānucarita | 2. Kapitel | A.

manor vaivasvatasyasan putrā vai nava tatsamāḥ. |
iksvākuś caiva nābhāgo dhṛṣṭāḥ śaryātir eva ca || 1 ||
narīṣyantas tathā prāṃśur nābhāgāriṣṭasaptamāḥ |
karūṣaś ca pṛṣadhrāś ca navaite mānavāḥ smṛtāḥ. || 2 ||
akarot putrakāmas tu manur iṣṭiṃ prajāpatiḥ |
| mitrā-varuṇayor viprāḥ pūrvam eva mahāmatiḥ || 3 ||
| anutpanneṣu navasu putreṣv eteṣu bho dvijāḥ. |
| tasyāṃ ca vartamānāyām iṣṭyāṃ ca dvijasattamāḥ || 4 ||
mitrā-varuṇayor aṃśe manur āhutim ājuhot |
tatra divyāmbaradharā divyābharaṇabhūṣitā || 5 ||
divyasam̃hananā caiva ilā jajña iti śrutiḥ. |
tām ilety eva hovāca manur daṇḍadbaras tadā: || 6 ||
anugacchasva māṃ bhadre! tam ilā pratyuvāca ha |
dharmayuktam idaṃ vākyaṃ putrakāmaṃ prajāpatim: || 7 ||
ilovāca: mitrā-varuṇayor am̃se jātāsmi vadatāṃ vara, |
tayoḥ sakāśaṃ yāsyāmi, mā no dharmo hato vadhīt! || 8 ||
saivam uktvā manuṃ devaṃ |
mitrā-varuṇayor ilā |
gatvāntikaṃ varārohā prāñjalir vākyam abravīt: || 9 ||
am̃se 'smi yuvayor jātā, devau, kiṃ karavāṇi vāṃ? |
manunaiṣvāham uktāsmi: anugacchasva mām iti! || 10 ||
tāṃ tathāvādinīṃ sādhvīṃ ilāṃ dharmaparāyaṇāṃ |
mitrās ca varuṇās cobhāv ūcatus tāṃ dvijottamāḥ: || 11 ||
anena tava dharmajñe praśrayeṇa damena ca |
satyena caiva suśroṇi prītau svo varavarṇini. || 12 ||
ā vayos tvaṃ mahābhāge khyātiṃ kanyeti yāsyasi, |
| manor vaṃśakarāḥ putras tvam eva ca bhaviṣyasi || 13 ||
sudyumna iti vikhyātas triṣu lokeṣu pūjitaḥ |
jagatpriyo dharmaśīlo manor vaṃśavivardhanaḥ. || 14 ||
nivṛttā sā tu tac chrutvā | sā tu devī varaṃ labdhvā gacchantī pitur antikaṃ | nivṛttā pitaraṃ prati |
budhenāntaram āsādyā maithunāyopamanritā. || 15 ||
somaputrād budhāc cāsyam ailo jajñe purūravāḥ. |
janayitvā tataḥ sā taṃ | budhāt sā janayitvā tu ilā sudyumnatāṃ gatā. || 16 || sudyumnatvaṃ punar gatā. || 16 ||
sudyumnasya tu dāyādās trayaḥ paramadhārmikāḥ: |
utkalās ca gayas caiva vinatāsvas tathaiva ca. || 17 ||
utkalasy-otkalaṃ rāṣṭraṃ, vinatāsvasya paścimaṃ, |
dik pūrvā tasya rājarṣer, gayasya tu gayā purī. || 18 ||
praviṣṭe tu manau tasmin prajāḥ sṛṣtvā divakaraṃ |
daśadhā tad dadhat kṣatram akarot pṛthivīm imāṃ. || 19 ||
iksvākur jyeṣṭhadāyādo madhyadeśam avāptavān. |
kanyābhāvāt tu sudyumno naiva bhāgam avāptavān. || 20 ||
vasiṣṭhavacanāt tv āsīt pratiṣṭhāne mahādyutiḥ |
pratiṣṭhā dharmarājasya sudyumnasya mahātmanāḥ. || 21 ||
tat purūravase prādād rājyaṃ prāpya mahāyaśāḥ |
mānaveyo mahābhagāḥ strīpumsor lakṣaṇair yutaḥ || 22 ||
dhṛtavān ya ilety evaṃ sudyumneti ca viśrutaḥ. |
etac chrutvā tu ṛṣayaḥ papracchus tadanantaraṃ |
mānavāḥ sa tu sudyumnāḥ stribhāvam agamat kathaṃ? || 22 ||
sūta uvāca: purā maheśvaraṃ draṣṭuṃ kumārās sauakādayāḥ |
ilāvṛtaṃ samājagmur dadṛṣur vṛṣabhadhvajaṃ. || 22 ||
umayā ramamāṇaṃ taṃ vilokya pihite sthale |
pratijagmus tataḥ sarve, vṛīditābhūc chivāpy atha. || 22 ||
provāca vacanaṃ devī priyahetoḥ priyaṃ priyā: |
imaṃ mamāśramaṃ deva yaḥ puinān saṃpravekṣyati, || 224 ||
bhaviṣyati dhruvaṃ nārī sa tulyāpsarasāṃ śubhā. |
tatra sarvāḥ bhūtāni piśācaḥ paśavaś ca ye, || 225 ||
stribūtāḥ saha rudreṇa kriḍanty apsaraso yathā. |
umāvanaṃ praviṣṭas tu sa rājā mṛgayāṃ gataḥ || 226 ||
piśācaḥ saha bhūtais tu rudre stribhāvam āsthite. |
tasmāt sa rājā sudyumnaḥ stribhāvaṃ labdhavān punaḥ |
mahādevaprasādāc ca mānavatvam avāptavān. || 227 ||
nāriṣyantāḥ śakāḥ putrā, nābhāgasya tu bho dvijāḥ || 23 ||
ambariṣo 'bhavat putraḥ pārthivarsabhasattamaḥ, |
dhṛṣṭasya dhārṣṭakaṃ kṣatraṃ raṇadṛṣṭaṃ babhūva ha. || 24 ||
nisargaṃ manuputrāṇāṃ vistareṇa nibodhata ! |
pṛṣadbro himsayitvā tu guror gāṃ niṣy abhakṣayat || 24 ||
śapāc chūdratvam āpannaś cyavanasya mahātmanaḥ. |
karūṣasya tu kārūṣāḥ kṣatriyā yuddhadurmadāḥ, || 24 ||
sahasrakṣatriyagaṇo vikrāntaḥ saṃbabhūva ha. |
nābhāgodīṣṭaputras tu vidvān āsid bhalandanaḥ. || 24 ||
bhalandanasya putro 'bhūt prāṃsur nāma mahābalaḥ, |
prāṃsur eko 'bhavat putraḥ prajānir iti viśrutaḥ. || 244 ||
prajāner abhavat putraḥ khanitro nāma vīryavān, |
tasya putro 'bhavac chrīmān kṣupo nāma mahāyaśāḥ. || 245 ||
kṣupasya viṃśaḥ putras tu pratimānaṃ babhūva ha, |
viṃśaputras tu kalyāṇo vivimṣo nāma dhārmikaḥ. || 246 ||
vivimṣaputro dharmātmā khaninetraḥ pratāpavān, |
kharandhamas tasya putras tretāyugamukhe 'bhavat. || 247 ||
kharandhamasutās cāpi āvikṣin nāma vīryavān, |
āvikṣito vyatīkrāmat (!) pitaraṃ guṇavattayā. || 248 ||
marutto nāma dharmātmā cakravartisamo nṛpaḥ, |
saṃvartena divaṃ nītaḥ sasuhṛt saha bāndhavaḥ. || 249 ||
vivādo 'tra mahān āsit saṃvartasya bṛhaspateḥ, |
ṛddhiṃ dṛṣṭvā tu yajñasya kruddhas tasya bṛhaspatiḥ. || 2410 ||
saṃvartena hṛte yajñe cukopa subhṛṣaṃ tadā, |
lokānāṃ sa hi nāsāya daivatair hi prasāditaḥ. || 2411 ||
maruttās cakravartī sa narīṣyantam avāptavān, |
narīṣyantasya dāyādo rājā daṇḍadhāro damaḥ. || 2412 ||
tasya putras tu vikrānto rājāsid rāṣṭravardhanaḥ, |
sudhṛtis tasya putras tu, naraḥ sudhṛtitaḥ punaḥ. || 2413 ||
kevalas tasya putras tu, bandhumān kevalātmajaḥ, |
atha bandhumataḥ putro dharmātmā vegavān nṛpaḥ. || 2414 ||
budho vegavataḥ putras, tṛṇabindur budhātmaḥ, |
tretāyugamukhe rājā tṛṭiye saṃbabhūva ha. || 2415 ||
kaṇyā tu tasya dravidā matā viśrāvaso hi sā, |
putrās cāsyā viśālo 'bhūd rājā paramadhārmikaḥ || 2416 ||
dāsvān prakhyātavīryaujā, viśālā yena nirmitā, |
viśālasya suto rājā hemacandro mahābalaḥ. || 2417 ||
sucandra iti vikhyāto hemacandrād anantaraḥ, |
sucandra-tanayo rāja dhūmrāśva iti viśrutaḥ. || 2418 ||
dhūmrāśva-tanayo vidvān śṛñjayaḥ samapadyata, |
śṛñjayasya sutaḥ śrīmān sahadevaḥ pratāpavān. || 2419 ||
kṛśāśvaḥ sahadevasya putraḥ paramadhārmikaḥ, |
kṛśāśvasya mahātejāḥ somadattaḥ pratāpavān || 2420 ||
somadattasya rājarseḥ suto 'bhūj janamejayaḥ |
janamejayātmajaś caiva pramātir nāma viśrutaḥ. || 2421 ||
tṛṇabinduprasādena sarve vaiśālakā nṛpāḥ |
dirghāyuṣo mahātmāno viryavantaḥ sudhārmikāḥ. || 2422 ||
śaryāter mithunaṃ tv āsīd: ānarto nāma viśrutaḥ |
putraḥ sukanyā kanyā ca, yā patnī cyavanasya ha. || 25 ||
ānartasya tu dāyādo revo nāma mahadyutiḥ, |
ānarta-viṣayaś cāsit purī cāsyā kuśasthalī. || 26 ||
revasya raivataḥ putraḥ kakudmī nāma dhārmikaḥ |
jyeṣṭhaḥ putraśatasyāsid rājyaṃ prāpya kuśasthalīṃ. || 27 ||
sa kanyāsahitaḥ śrutvā gāndharvaṃ brahmano 'ntike |
muhūrtabhūtaṃ devasya martyaṃ bahuyugaṃ vibhoḥ || 28 ||
ājagāma yuvaivātha svāṃ purīṃ yādavair vṛtāṃ |
krītāṃ dvāravatīṃ nāma bahudvārāṃ manoramāṃ || 29 ||
bhoja-vṛṣṇy-andhakair guptāṃ vāsudevapurogamaiḥ. |
tatas tad raivato jñātvā | tāṃ kathāṃ raivataḥ śrutvā yathātattvaṃ dvijottamāḥ || 30 || yathātattvam arimdamaḥ || 30 ||
kanyāṃ tāṃ baladevāya suvratāṃ nāma revatīṃ |
dattvā jagāma śikharaṃ meros tapasi samsthitaḥ. || 31 ||
reme rāmo 'pi dharmātmā revatyā sahitaḥ sukhī. |
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ: | tāṃ kathāṃ ṛṣayaḥ śrutvā papracchus tadanantaram: || 32 ||
kathaṃ bahuyuge kāle samatite mahāmate |
na jarā revatīṃ prāptā raivataṃ vā kakudminaṃ, || 33 ||
meruṃ gatasya vā tasya śaryāteḥ samtatiḥ kathaṃ |
sthitā pṛthivyām adyāpi, śrotum icchāma tattvataḥ! || 34 ||
sūta uvāca: na jarā kṣutpipāsā vā na ca mṛtyubhayaṃ tataḥ, |
ṛtu cakraṃ na bhavati | na ca rogaḥ prabhavati brahmaloke sadānaghāḥ. || 35 || brahmaloḥ gatasya ha. || 35 ||
kakudminas tu taṃ lokaṃ raivatasya gatasya ha |
batā puṇyajanaiḥ sarvā rākṣasaiḥ sā kuśasthali. || 36 ||
tasya bhrātrīśataṃ tv āśīd dhārmikasya mahātmanaḥ, |
tad vadhyamānaṃ rakṣobhir diśaḥ saṃprādravad bhayāt. || 37 ||
vidrutasya ca viprendrās tasya bhrātrīśatasya vai |
teṣāṃ tu te bhayākrāntāḥ kṣatriyās tatra tatra ha |
anvavāyas tu sumahāms tatra tatra dvijottamāḥ, || 38 ||
yeṣāṃ hy ete munīśreṣṭhāḥ śāryātā iti viśrutāḥ |
kṣatriyā guṇasaṃpannā dikṣu sarvāsu viśrutāḥ, || 39 ||
sarvaśaḥ sarvagahanaṃ praviṣṭās te mahaujasaḥ. |
nabhāgāriṣṭaputrau dvau vaiśyau brāhmanatāṃ gatau. || 40 ||
karūṣasya tu kārūṣāḥ kṣatriyā yuddhadurmadāḥ. |
śāryātā iti vikhyātā dikṣu sarvāsu dhārmikāḥ. || 39 ||
dhṛṣṭasya dhārṣṭakaṃ sarvaṃ raṇadṛṣṭaṃ babhūva ha, |
trisahasraṃ tu sa gaṇaḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ mahātmanāṃ. || 40 ||
nabhagasya ca dāyādo nabhāgo nāma vīryavān, |
pṛśadbro hiṃsayitvā tu guror gāṃ dvijasattamāḥ || 41 ||
śapāc chūdratvam āpanno, navaite parikirtitāḥ |
vaivasvatasya tanayā manor vai munisattamāḥ. || 42 ||
ambarīśas tu nābhāgir, virūpas tasya cātmajāḥ. || 41 ||
pṛśadaśvo virūpasya, tasya putro rathītarāḥ. |
ete kṣatraprasūtā vai punaś c-āṅgirasāḥ smṛtāḥ, |
rathītarāṇāṃ pravarāḥ kṣātropetā dvijātayāḥ. || 42 ||
kṣuvatas tu manoḥ pūrvam iksvākur abhavaṭ sutāḥ, |
tasya putraśataṃ tv āsid iksvākor bhūridakṣiṇaṃ. || 43 ||
